# ===== airing_scheduler_v1120.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["airing_scheduler_v1120"] = (
    "\"\"\"光鸭转存助手 v1.12.0：每日助手逐集日历驱动的 due_missing 调度层。\n"
    "\n"
    "核心目标：\n"
    "- 不再把 MoviePilot 的“总缺集”直接当成“现在就该搜索的集”；\n"
    "- 优先读取运行中的每日助手整季日历，缺失时用 MoviePilot TMDB season detail 回退；\n"
    "- 普通后台轮次只允许 ``due_missing`` 进入光鸭/迅雷/Magnet/ED2K；未来集不访问外部资源站；\n"
    "- 提前 12 小时进入到期窗口，兼顾资源偷跑；每天一次全员复核仍允许完整缺集搜索；\n"
    "- 同订阅整条来源链共用 v1.10.24 的 RLock，修复“刚打印冷却、几秒后另一线程又搜索”的竞争；\n"
    "- 修复旧分享 ``handled=True`` 被误当成“全部缺集已覆盖”：只要 due gap 仍存在就继续 Magnet/ED2K。\n"
    "\n"
    "日期精度说明：TMDB 标准季详情只有 air_date 时，使用本机时区的默认 20:00 作为估算上映时刻；\n"
    "数据源若提供 air_at 则按精确时间执行。估算值明确标记 precision=date，不伪装成官方精确时刻。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import datetime\n"
    "from contextlib import contextmanager, nullcontext\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Set\n"
    "\n"
    "from app.chain.media import MediaChain\n"
    "from app.schemas.types import MediaSource, MediaType\n"
    "from app.sdk.plugins import PluginManager\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaAiringSchedulerV1120Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"更新日历 -> due_missing -> 来源执行的最外层调度权威。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    plugin_version = \"1.12.0\"\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260903-r44\"\n"
    "    _calendar_refresh_hours_v1120 = 6\n"
    "    _calendar_default_hour_v1120 = 20\n"
    "    _calendar_early_hours_v1120 = 12\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        config = dict(config or {})\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self._calendar_default_hour_v1120 = max(0, min(23, int(config.get(\"calendar_default_hour\", 20) or 20)))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            self._calendar_default_hour_v1120 = 20\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self._calendar_early_hours_v1120 = max(0, min(72, int(config.get(\"calendar_early_hours\", 12) or 12)))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            self._calendar_early_hours_v1120 = 12\n"
    "        self._airing_due_scope_v1120: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None\n"
    "        self._airing_due_force_v1120 = False\n"
    "        return super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _calendar_value_v1120(obj: Any, field: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:\n"
    "        if isinstance(obj, dict):\n"
    "            return obj.get(field, default)\n"
    "        return getattr(obj, field, default)\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _positive_int_set_v1120(values: Iterable[Any]) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        result: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        for raw in values or []:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                value = int(raw or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if value > 0:\n"
    "                result.add(value)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _calendar_requests_v1120(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        selected = {\n"
    "            int(value) for value in (getattr(self, \"_selected_subscriptions\", []) or [])\n"
    "            if str(value).isdigit() and int(value) > 0\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for subscribe in self._list_subscriptions(None) or []:\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if sid not in selected or self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if str(getattr(subscribe, \"state\", \"\") or \"\") not in {\"N\", \"R\"}:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            tmdb_id = str(self._subscription_tmdb_id_v1110(subscribe) or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if not tmdb_id.isdigit():\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                season = int(getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0) or 1)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                season = 1\n"
    "            rows.append({\n"
    "                \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                \"title\": str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"year\": str(getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"tmdb_id\": tmdb_id,\n"
    "                \"season\": max(1, season),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _calendar_episode_rows_v1120(detail: Any, season: int) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        episodes = detail.get(\"episodes\") if isinstance(detail, dict) else getattr(detail, \"episodes\", [])\n"
    "        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for raw in episodes or []:\n"
    "            getter = raw.get if isinstance(raw, dict) else lambda key, default=None: getattr(raw, key, default)\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                episode = int(getter(\"episode_number\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                episode = 0\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                season_number = int(getter(\"season_number\", season) or season)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                season_number = season\n"
    "            if episode <= 0 or season_number != season:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            air_date = str(getter(\"air_date\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            air_at = str(\n"
    "                getter(\"air_at\", \"\")\n"
    "                or getter(\"air_datetime\", \"\")\n"
    "                or getter(\"release_at\", \"\")\n"
    "                or \"\"\n"
    "            ).strip()\n"
    "            if \"T\" in air_date and not air_at:\n"
    "                air_at = air_date\n"
    "                air_date = air_date[:10]\n"
    "            elif air_date:\n"
    "                air_date = air_date[:10]\n"
    "            rows.append({\n"
    "                \"episode\": episode,\n"
    "                \"season\": season_number,\n"
    "                \"air_date\": air_date,\n"
    "                \"air_at\": air_at,\n"
    "                \"precision\": \"datetime\" if air_at else (\"date\" if air_date else \"unknown\"),\n"
    "                \"name\": str(getter(\"name\", \"\") or \"\")[:180],\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        rows.sort(key=lambda row: int(row.get(\"episode\") or 0))\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _calendar_local_fallback_v1120(self, request: Dict[str, Any], force: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        chain = MediaChain()\n"
    "        tmdb_id = str(request.get(\"tmdb_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "        season = int(request.get(\"season\") or 1)\n"
    "        episodes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        error = \"\"\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            detail = chain.tmdb_info(tmdbid=int(tmdb_id), mtype=MediaType.TV, season=season)\n"
    "            episodes = self._calendar_episode_rows_v1120(detail or {}, season)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            error = str(err)[:220]\n"
    "        if not episodes:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    recognize = getattr(self, \"_recognize_media_cached_v208\", None)\n"
    "                    if callable(recognize):\n"
    "                        info = recognize(\n"
    "                            mtype=MediaType.TV,\n"
    "                            media_source=MediaSource.TMDB,\n"
    "                            media_id=tmdb_id,\n"
    "                            cache=not force,\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                    else:\n"
    "                        info = chain.recognize_media(\n"
    "                            mtype=MediaType.TV,\n"
    "                            media_source=MediaSource.TMDB,\n"
    "                            media_id=tmdb_id,\n"
    "                            cache=not force,\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                except TypeError:\n"
    "                    recognize = getattr(self, \"_recognize_media_cached_v208\", None)\n"
    "                    if callable(recognize):\n"
    "                        info = recognize(\n"
    "                            mtype=MediaType.TV,\n"
    "                            media_source=MediaSource.TMDB,\n"
    "                            media_id=tmdb_id,\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                    else:\n"
    "                        info = chain.recognize_media(\n"
    "                            mtype=MediaType.TV,\n"
    "                            media_source=MediaSource.TMDB,\n"
    "                            media_id=tmdb_id,\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                info = None\n"
    "                error = error or str(err)[:220]\n"
    "            next_episode = getattr(info, \"next_episode_to_air\", None) if info else None\n"
    "            if next_episode:\n"
    "                episodes = self._calendar_episode_rows_v1120({\"episodes\": [next_episode]}, season)\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            **request,\n"
    "            \"episodes\": episodes,\n"
    "            \"episode_count\": len(episodes),\n"
    "            \"provider\": \"guangya_tmdb_fallback\",\n"
    "            \"fetched_at\": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n"
    "            \"error\": error,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _calendar_stale_v1120(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            updated = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(str(payload.get(\"updated_at\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "        return datetime.datetime.now() - updated >= datetime.timedelta(\n"
    "            hours=GuangYaAiringSchedulerV1120Mixin._calendar_refresh_hours_v1120\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dailyassistant_calendar_v1120(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        requests: List[Dict[str, Any]],\n"
    "        force: bool,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            manager = PluginManager()\n"
    "            running = getattr(manager, \"running_plugins\", None) or {}\n"
    "            daily = running.get(\"DailyAssistant\") if isinstance(running, dict) else None\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            daily = None\n"
    "        provider = getattr(daily, \"get_airing_schedule_snapshot\", None) if daily else None\n"
    "        if not callable(provider):\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            snapshot = dict(provider(requests=requests, force=force) or {})\n"
    "        except TypeError:\n"
    "            snapshot = dict(provider(requests, force=force) or {})\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【更新日历】读取每日助手逐集日历失败，回退 MoviePilot TMDB：%s\", err)\n"
    "            return {}\n"
    "        result: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        for item in snapshot.get(\"items\") or []:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(item, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            sid = int(item.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0)\n"
    "            if sid > 0:\n"
    "                result[sid] = dict(item)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(self, force: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        current = self.get_data(\"airing_calendar_v1120\") or {}\n"
    "        if (\n"
    "            not force\n"
    "            and isinstance(current, dict)\n"
    "            and current.get(\"subscriptions\")\n"
    "            and not self._calendar_stale_v1120(current)\n"
    "        ):\n"
    "            return current\n"
    "\n"
    "        requests = self._calendar_requests_v1120()\n"
    "        daily_rows = self._dailyassistant_calendar_v1120(requests, force=force)\n"
    "        subscriptions: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        daily_count = fallback_count = 0\n"
    "        errors: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for request in requests:\n"
    "            sid = int(request.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0)\n"
    "            item = daily_rows.get(sid)\n"
    "            if item and list(item.get(\"episodes\") or []):\n"
    "                row = {**request, **item}\n"
    "                row[\"provider\"] = str(item.get(\"provider\") or \"dailyassistant\")\n"
    "                daily_count += 1\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                row = self._calendar_local_fallback_v1120(request, force=force)\n"
    "                fallback_count += 1\n"
    "            if row.get(\"error\"):\n"
    "                errors.append(f\"#{sid} {request.get('title')}: {row.get('error')}\")\n"
    "            subscriptions.append(row)\n"
    "\n"
    "        payload = {\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n"
    "            \"count\": len(subscriptions),\n"
    "            \"dailyassistant\": daily_count,\n"
    "            \"fallback\": fallback_count,\n"
    "            \"subscriptions\": subscriptions,\n"
    "            \"errors\": errors[:30],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"airing_calendar_v1120\", payload)\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【更新日历v2】逐集日历刷新：订阅=%s 每日助手=%s TMDB回退=%s 异常=%s\",\n"
    "            len(subscriptions), daily_count, fallback_count, len(errors),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return payload\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _raw_subscription_missing_v1120(self, subscribe: Any) -> List[int]:\n"
    "        previous = getattr(self, \"_airing_due_scope_v1120\", None)\n"
    "        self._airing_due_scope_v1120 = None\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return sorted(self._positive_int_set_v1120(super()._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or []))\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            self._airing_due_scope_v1120 = previous\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _subscription_missing_episodes(self, subscribe: Any) -> List[int]:\n"
    "        values = sorted(self._positive_int_set_v1120(super()._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or []))\n"
    "        scope = getattr(self, \"_airing_due_scope_v1120\", None)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(scope, dict):\n"
    "            return values\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        if sid != int(scope.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0):\n"
    "            return values\n"
    "        allowed = self._positive_int_set_v1120(scope.get(\"episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        return [value for value in values if value in allowed]\n"
    "\n"
    "    @contextmanager\n"
    "    def _due_scope_v1120(self, subscribe: Any, episodes: Iterable[int]):\n"
    "        previous = getattr(self, \"_airing_due_scope_v1120\", None)\n"
    "        self._airing_due_scope_v1120 = {\n"
    "            \"subscribe_id\": int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0),\n"
    "            \"episodes\": sorted(self._positive_int_set_v1120(episodes)),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            yield\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            self._airing_due_scope_v1120 = previous\n"
    "\n"
    "    @contextmanager\n"
    "    def _without_due_scope_v1120(self):\n"
    "        previous = getattr(self, \"_airing_due_scope_v1120\", None)\n"
    "        self._airing_due_scope_v1120 = None\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            yield\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            self._airing_due_scope_v1120 = previous\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _sync_media_facts_progress(self, subscribe: Any) -> int:\n"
    "        with self._without_due_scope_v1120():\n"
    "            return super()._sync_media_facts_progress(subscribe)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _finish_subscription_if_complete(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        channel_state: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n"
    "    ) -> bool:\n"
    "        \"\"\"退出 due scope 做真实完成检查，并完整保留 legacy 的 channel_state ABI。\"\"\"\n"
    "        with self._without_due_scope_v1120():\n"
    "            return bool(\n"
    "                super()._finish_subscription_if_complete(\n"
    "                    subscribe,\n"
    "                    channel_state=channel_state,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _commit_episode_receipt_v1124(self, subscribe: Any, episodes: Iterable[int], origin: str) -> None:\n"
    "        with self._without_due_scope_v1120():\n"
    "            return super()._commit_episode_receipt_v1124(subscribe, episodes, origin)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _episode_air_at_v1120(self, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[datetime.datetime]:\n"
    "        raw_at = str(row.get(\"air_at\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if raw_at:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                parsed = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(raw_at.replace(\"Z\", \"+00:00\"))\n"
    "                if parsed.tzinfo is not None:\n"
    "                    parsed = parsed.astimezone().replace(tzinfo=None)\n"
    "                return parsed\n"
    "            except ValueError:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "        raw_date = str(row.get(\"air_date\") or \"\").strip()[:10]\n"
    "        if not raw_date:\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            day = datetime.date.fromisoformat(raw_date)\n"
    "        except ValueError:\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        return datetime.datetime.combine(\n"
    "            day,\n"
    "            datetime.time(hour=int(getattr(self, \"_calendar_default_hour_v1120\", 20) or 20)),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _calendar_item_for_v1120(self, subscribe: Any, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        for item in payload.get(\"subscriptions\") or []:\n"
    "            if isinstance(item, dict) and int(item.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) == sid:\n"
    "                return dict(item)\n"
    "        return None\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _airing_gate_v1120(self, subscribe: Any, payload: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        raw_missing = set(self._raw_subscription_missing_v1120(subscribe))\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        if not raw_missing:\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                \"calendar_available\": True,\n"
    "                \"raw_missing\": [],\n"
    "                \"due_missing\": [],\n"
    "                \"due_uncovered\": [],\n"
    "                \"future_missing\": [],\n"
    "                \"unscheduled_missing\": [],\n"
    "                \"covered\": True,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        calendar = payload or self._refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(force=False)\n"
    "        item = self._calendar_item_for_v1120(subscribe, calendar)\n"
    "        episodes = list((item or {}).get(\"episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        now = datetime.datetime.now()\n"
    "        early = datetime.timedelta(hours=int(getattr(self, \"_calendar_early_hours_v1120\", 12) or 12))\n"
    "        scheduled: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        due: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        future: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        for row in episodes:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                episode = int(row.get(\"episode\") or row.get(\"episode_number\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if episode <= 0 or episode not in raw_missing:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            air_at = self._episode_air_at_v1120(row)\n"
    "            if not air_at:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            normalized = dict(row)\n"
    "            normalized[\"air_at_effective\"] = air_at.isoformat(timespec=\"minutes\")\n"
    "            normalized[\"early_at\"] = (air_at - early).isoformat(timespec=\"minutes\")\n"
    "            scheduled[episode] = normalized\n"
    "            if now >= air_at - early:\n"
    "                due.add(episode)\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                future.add(episode)\n"
    "\n"
    "        unscheduled = set(raw_missing) - set(scheduled)\n"
    "        # 若更大的集号已经进入应播窗口，则更小但缺日期的集不应被“未知排期”永久挡住。\n"
    "        max_due = max(due) if due else 0\n"
    "        implied_due = {episode for episode in unscheduled if max_due and episode <= max_due}\n"
    "        due.update(implied_due)\n"
    "        unscheduled -= implied_due\n"
    "\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            reservations = dict(self._pending_reservations(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "            reserved = self._positive_int_set_v1120(reservations.get(\"episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            reserved = set()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            claimed = self._positive_int_set_v1120(self._active_source_claims(sid) or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            claimed = set()\n"
    "        due_uncovered = due - reserved - claimed\n"
    "\n"
    "        next_episode = 0\n"
    "        next_air_at = \"\"\n"
    "        next_precision = \"\"\n"
    "        # next future must come from future only (air_at > now), never from past due episodes.\n"
    "        future_candidates = [\n"
    "            (episode, self._episode_air_at_v1120(row), str(row.get(\"precision\") or \"date\"))\n"
    "            for episode, row in scheduled.items()\n"
    "            if episode in future\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        future_candidates = [row for row in future_candidates if row[1] is not None and row[1] > now]\n"
    "        if future_candidates:\n"
    "            episode, air_at, precision = min(future_candidates, key=lambda row: (row[1], row[0]))\n"
    "            next_episode = int(episode)\n"
    "            next_air_at = air_at.isoformat(timespec=\"minutes\") if air_at else \"\"\n"
    "            next_precision = precision\n"
    "\n"
    "        result = {\n"
    "            \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "            \"name\": str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"calendar_available\": bool(scheduled),\n"
    "            \"calendar_provider\": str((item or {}).get(\"provider\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"raw_missing\": sorted(raw_missing),\n"
    "            \"due_missing\": sorted(due),\n"
    "            \"due_uncovered\": sorted(due_uncovered),\n"
    "            \"reserved\": sorted(reserved.intersection(raw_missing)),\n"
    "            \"claimed\": sorted(claimed.intersection(raw_missing)),\n"
    "            \"future_missing\": sorted(future),\n"
    "            \"unscheduled_missing\": sorted(unscheduled),\n"
    "            \"next_episode\": next_episode,\n"
    "            \"next_air_at\": next_air_at,\n"
    "            \"next_precision\": next_precision,\n"
    "            \"early_hours\": int(getattr(self, \"_calendar_early_hours_v1120\", 12) or 12),\n"
    "            \"default_hour\": int(getattr(self, \"_calendar_default_hour_v1120\", 20) or 20),\n"
    "            \"checked_at\": now.isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        state = self.get_data(\"airing_gate_state_v1120\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(state, dict):\n"
    "            state = {}\n"
    "        previous = dict(state.get(str(sid)) or {})\n"
    "        state[str(sid)] = result\n"
    "        if len(state) > 1000:\n"
    "            state = dict(list(state.items())[-1000:])\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"airing_gate_state_v1120\", state)\n"
    "        signature_fields = (\"due_uncovered\", \"future_missing\", \"unscheduled_missing\", \"next_episode\", \"next_air_at\")\n"
    "        if any(previous.get(key) != result.get(key) for key in signature_fields):\n"
    "            if result[\"due_uncovered\"]:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【更新日历v2】#%s %s 当前应补=%s；未来=%s；未排期=%s\",\n"
    "                    sid,\n"
    "                    result[\"name\"],\n"
    "                    \",\".join(f\"E{v:02d}\" for v in result[\"due_uncovered\"]) or \"无\",\n"
    "                    \",\".join(f\"E{v:02d}\" for v in result[\"future_missing\"]) or \"无\",\n"
    "                    \",\".join(f\"E{v:02d}\" for v in result[\"unscheduled_missing\"]) or \"无\",\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【更新日历v2】#%s %s 已追平当前播出进度；未来=%s；下一集=E%s @ %s precision=%s，本轮不访问外部资源站\",\n"
    "                    sid,\n"
    "                    result[\"name\"],\n"
    "                    \",\".join(f\"E{v:02d}\" for v in result[\"future_missing\"]) or \"无\",\n"
    "                    f\"{next_episode:02d}\" if next_episode else \"--\",\n"
    "                    next_air_at or \"未知\",\n"
    "                    next_precision or \"unknown\",\n"
    "                )\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _refresh_airing_calendar_v1110(self, force: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"兼容旧页面合同：v1.11.0 卡片继续显示每个订阅的下一缺集，但底层来自整季日历。\"\"\"\n"
    "        full = self._refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(force=force)\n"
    "        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for item in full.get(\"subscriptions\") or []:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(item, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            sid = int(item.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0)\n"
    "            subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid) if sid else None\n"
    "            if not subscribe:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            missing = set(self._raw_subscription_missing_v1120(subscribe))\n"
    "            scheduled = []\n"
    "            for episode_row in item.get(\"episodes\") or []:\n"
    "                if not isinstance(episode_row, dict):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                episode = int(episode_row.get(\"episode\") or 0)\n"
    "                if episode not in missing:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                air_at = self._episode_air_at_v1120(episode_row)\n"
    "                if not air_at:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                scheduled.append((air_at, episode, episode_row))\n"
    "            if not scheduled:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            air_at, episode, episode_row = min(scheduled, key=lambda row: (row[0], row[1]))\n"
    "            rows.append({\n"
    "                \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                \"name\": str(item.get(\"title\") or getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"year\": str(item.get(\"year\") or getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"tmdb_id\": str(item.get(\"tmdb_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"season\": int(item.get(\"season\") or getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0) or 1),\n"
    "                \"episode\": episode,\n"
    "                \"air_date\": str(episode_row.get(\"air_date\") or air_at.date().isoformat()),\n"
    "                \"air_at\": air_at.isoformat(timespec=\"minutes\"),\n"
    "                \"precision\": str(episode_row.get(\"precision\") or \"date\"),\n"
    "                \"missing\": sorted(missing),\n"
    "                \"target_missing\": True,\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        rows.sort(key=lambda row: (str(row.get(\"air_at\") or row.get(\"air_date\") or \"\"), int(row.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0)))\n"
    "        payload = {\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": str(full.get(\"updated_at\") or datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")),\n"
    "            \"count\": len(rows),\n"
    "            \"items\": rows,\n"
    "            \"errors\": list(full.get(\"errors\") or [])[:30],\n"
    "            \"engine\": \"v1.12.0-full-season-calendar\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"airing_calendar_v1110\", payload)\n"
    "        return payload\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _calendar_due_check_v1110(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"每小时只检查真正进入提前窗口的订阅；同一订阅仍至少间隔两小时。\"\"\"\n"
    "        payload = self._refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(force=False)\n"
    "        state = self.get_data(\"airing_check_state_v1110\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(state, dict):\n"
    "            state = {}\n"
    "        now = datetime.datetime.now()\n"
    "        checked = skipped = 0\n"
    "        results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for item in payload.get(\"subscriptions\") or []:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(item, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            sid = int(item.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0)\n"
    "            subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid) if sid else None\n"
    "            if not subscribe or not self._is_guangya_route(subscribe):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            gate = self._airing_gate_v1120(subscribe, payload=payload)\n"
    "            if not gate.get(\"due_uncovered\"):\n"
    "                skipped += 1\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            row_state = state.get(str(sid)) if isinstance(state.get(str(sid)), dict) else {}\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                last = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(str(row_state.get(\"checked_at\") or \"\"))\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                last = None\n"
    "            cooldown_hours = int(getattr(self, \"_calendar_per_sub_cooldown_hours_v1110\", 2) or 2)\n"
    "            if last and now - last < datetime.timedelta(hours=cooldown_hours):\n"
    "                skipped += 1\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            previous_force = bool(getattr(self, \"_airing_due_force_v1120\", False))\n"
    "            self._airing_due_force_v1120 = True\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                result = dict(self._try_transfer_subscription(subscribe, force=True, refresh_channel=False) or {})\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                result = {\"success\": False, \"message\": str(err)[:300]}\n"
    "            finally:\n"
    "                self._airing_due_force_v1120 = previous_force\n"
    "            state[str(sid)] = {\n"
    "                \"checked_at\": now.isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n"
    "                \"episodes\": list(gate.get(\"due_uncovered\") or []),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            results.append({\n"
    "                \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                \"episodes\": list(gate.get(\"due_uncovered\") or []),\n"
    "                \"success\": bool(result.get(\"success\")),\n"
    "                \"message\": str(result.get(\"message\") or \"\")[:300],\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            checked += 1\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"airing_check_state_v1110\", state)\n"
    "        if checked:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"INFO\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【更新日历v2】到期检查完成：执行=%s 跳过=%s\", checked, skipped)\n"
    "        return {\"success\": True, \"checked\": checked, \"skipped\": skipped, \"results\": results}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _run_airing_subscription_v1120(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        force: bool,\n"
    "        refresh_channel: bool,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return dict(super()._try_transfer_subscription(\n"
    "                subscribe,\n"
    "                force=force,\n"
    "                refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "            ) or {})\n"
    "\n"
    "        due_force = bool(getattr(self, \"_airing_due_force_v1120\", False))\n"
    "        # 人工强制/每日全员复核保留一次完整补漏；日历到期服务虽然 force=True，仍只允许 due 集。\n"
    "        if force and not due_force:\n"
    "            return dict(super()._try_transfer_subscription(\n"
    "                subscribe,\n"
    "                force=True,\n"
    "                refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "            ) or {})\n"
    "\n"
    "        gate = self._airing_gate_v1120(subscribe)\n"
    "        if not bool(gate.get(\"calendar_available\")) and str(gate.get(\"decision\") or \"\") not in {\n"
    "            \"continue_match\", \"search\", \"search_due\",\n"
    "        }:\n"
    "            # 上游日历完全不可用时保持既有可靠性，不因第三方数据故障冻结追更。\n"
    "            result = dict(super()._try_transfer_subscription(\n"
    "                subscribe,\n"
    "                force=force,\n"
    "                refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "            ) or {})\n"
    "            result[\"calendar_gate\"] = gate\n"
    "            result[\"calendar_fallback_legacy\"] = True\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        decision = str(gate.get(\"decision\") or \"\")\n"
    "        # UNKNOWN / continue_match with concrete due targets only — never white-search.\n"
    "        if decision in {\"continue_match\", \"unknown_no_due_target\"} or str(gate.get(\"preflight_state\") or \"\") == \"UNKNOWN\":\n"
    "            due = list(gate.get(\"due_uncovered\") or gate.get(\"target_episodes\") or gate.get(\"final_target\") or [])\n"
    "            if not due or decision == \"unknown_no_due_target\":\n"
    "                return {\n"
    "                    \"success\": True,\n"
    "                    \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                    \"calendar_wait\": True,\n"
    "                    \"calendar_gate\": gate,\n"
    "                    \"message\": \"library/calendar UNKNOWN 且无明确 due target，跳过主动 GYING\",\n"
    "                    \"selected\": False,\n"
    "                }\n"
    "            with self._due_scope_v1120(subscribe, due):\n"
    "                result = dict(super()._try_transfer_subscription(\n"
    "                    subscribe,\n"
    "                    force=force,\n"
    "                    refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "                ) or {})\n"
    "            result[\"due_scope\"] = due\n"
    "            result[\"calendar_gate\"] = gate\n"
    "            result[\"calendar_continue_match\"] = True\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        due = list(gate.get(\"due_uncovered\") or gate.get(\"target_episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        if not due:\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                \"calendar_wait\": True,\n"
    "                \"calendar_gate\": gate,\n"
    "                \"message\": (\n"
    "                    \"已追平当前播出进度；\"\n"
    "                    f\"未来集={','.join('E%02d' % int(v) for v in (gate.get('future_missing') or [])) or '无'}；\"\n"
    "                    f\"下一未来集=E{int(gate.get('next_episode') or 0):02d} @ {gate.get('next_air_at') or '未知'}\"\n"
    "                ),\n"
    "            }\n"
    "\n"
    "        with self._due_scope_v1120(subscribe, due):\n"
    "            result = dict(super()._try_transfer_subscription(\n"
    "                subscribe,\n"
    "                force=force,\n"
    "                refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "            ) or {})\n"
    "        result[\"calendar_gate\"] = gate\n"
    "        result[\"due_scope\"] = due\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _try_transfer_subscription(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        force: bool = False,\n"
    "        refresh_channel: bool = True,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"把整条来源链放进同订阅 RLock，再执行日历门禁。\"\"\"\n"
    "        lock_getter = getattr(self, \"_episode_fence_lock_v1124\", None)\n"
    "        lock = lock_getter(subscribe) if callable(lock_getter) else nullcontext()\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            return self._run_airing_subscription_v1120(\n"
    "                subscribe,\n"
    "                force=force,\n"
    "                refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _try_transfer_subscription_inner(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        force: bool = False,\n"
    "        refresh_channel: bool = True,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"handled 只表示前序入口处理过，不再等价于 due gap 已全部覆盖。\"\"\"\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._try_transfer_subscription_inner(\n"
    "            subscribe,\n"
    "            force=force,\n"
    "            refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "        ) or {})\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe) or result.get(\"viewing_external\"):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "        if not bool(result.get(\"handled\")):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "        gap_method = getattr(self, \"_viewing_gap_v1113\", None)\n"
    "        if not callable(gap_method):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "        gap = dict(gap_method(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        if bool(gap.get(\"covered\")):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "        uncovered = list(gap.get(\"uncovered\") or [])\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【覆盖修正】#%s 前序 handled=True 但当前 due gap 仍未覆盖=%s；继续 Magnet/ED2K，而不是把‘旧链接已处理’误当成‘集数已齐’\",\n"
    "            int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0),\n"
    "            \",\".join(f\"E{int(v):02d}\" for v in uncovered) or \"movie\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            viewing = dict(self._dispatch_viewing_external_v1113(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            viewing = {\"success\": False, \"actions\": [], \"message\": str(err)[:300]}\n"
    "        if viewing.get(\"actions\"):\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                **result,\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                \"viewing_external\": viewing,\n"
    "                \"message\": f\"{str(result.get('message') or '前序来源已检查')}；{viewing.get('message') or '继续补齐 due gap'}\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        return {**result, \"viewing_external\": viewing}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaAiringSchedulerV1120Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["airing_ui_v1120"] = (
    "\"\"\"光鸭转存助手 v1.12.0：逐集日历配置 UI 与持久化保护。\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaAiringUiV1120Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"把日期估算时刻与提前检查窗口暴露给配置页，并防止旧异步保存覆盖。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260903-r44\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_form(self) -> Tuple[List[dict], Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        form, defaults = super().get_form()\n"
    "        defaults = dict(defaults or {})\n"
    "        defaults.setdefault(\"calendar_default_hour\", 20)\n"
    "        defaults.setdefault(\"calendar_early_hours\", 12)\n"
    "\n"
    "        section_builder = getattr(self, \"_section\", None)\n"
    "        field_builder = getattr(self, \"_field\", None)\n"
    "        if not callable(section_builder) or not callable(field_builder):\n"
    "            return form, defaults\n"
    "\n"
    "        calendar = section_builder(\n"
    "            \"更新日历\",\n"
    "            \"按每集上映日期计算当前应补集；未来集不再每轮访问频道、迅雷和观影。TMDB 只有日期时使用本地估算时刻。\",\n"
    "            \"mdi-calendar-clock-outline\",\n"
    "            [{\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VRow\",\n"
    "                \"content\": [\n"
    "                    field_builder(\n"
    "                        \"calendar_default_hour\",\n"
    "                        \"日期默认更新时间（本地小时）\",\n"
    "                        md=6,\n"
    "                        type=\"number\",\n"
    "                        min=\"0\",\n"
    "                        max=\"23\",\n"
    "                        hint=\"TMDB 只有 air_date 没有具体时刻时使用；默认 20:00。\",\n"
    "                        **{\"persistent-hint\": True},\n"
    "                    ),\n"
    "                    field_builder(\n"
    "                        \"calendar_early_hours\",\n"
    "                        \"提前检查窗口（小时）\",\n"
    "                        md=6,\n"
    "                        type=\"number\",\n"
    "                        min=\"0\",\n"
    "                        max=\"72\",\n"
    "                        hint=\"默认提前 12 小时允许搜索，兼顾资源提前放出；未来更远的集直接跳过。\",\n"
    "                        **{\"persistent-hint\": True},\n"
    "                    ),\n"
    "                ],\n"
    "            }],\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "        # ConfigUi v1.10 的最终结构为 VForm.content；放在接管设置之后、资源来源之前。\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            content = form[0][\"content\"]\n"
    "            if not any(\n"
    "                isinstance(row, dict)\n"
    "                and any(\n"
    "                    isinstance(child, dict) and child.get(\"text\") == \"更新日历\"\n"
    "                    for child in ((row.get(\"content\") or [{}])[0].get(\"content\") or [])\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                for row in content\n"
    "            ):\n"
    "                content.insert(2 if len(content) >= 2 else len(content), calendar)\n"
    "        except (IndexError, KeyError, TypeError):\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        return form, defaults\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _save_config(self) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"旧 planner 先完整保存，再把 v1.12.0 两个日历字段 merge 回整份配置。\"\"\"\n"
    "        super()._save_config()\n"
    "        config = self.get_config() or {}\n"
    "        config = dict(config) if isinstance(config, dict) else {}\n"
    "        config.update({\n"
    "            \"calendar_default_hour\": int(getattr(self, \"_calendar_default_hour_v1120\", 20) or 20),\n"
    "            \"calendar_early_hours\": int(getattr(self, \"_calendar_early_hours_v1120\", 12) or 12),\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        self.update_config(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaAiringUiV1120Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== airing_weekly_impl_v1121.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["airing_weekly_impl_v1121"] = (
    "\"\"\"光鸭转存助手 v1.12.1 预览：周视图追剧日历 + 星期级订阅门禁。\n"
    "\n"
    "- 剧集按逐集 air_date 放入周一至周日；普通后台只搜索当天应播订阅；\n"
    "- date 精度不允许提前窗口跨到前一天，精确 air_at 仍保留提前检查；\n"
    "- TMDB 下一集缺日期时，用最近已知集数的主要更新星期回退；\n"
    "- 电影没有固定星期，不进入星期筛选，继续由新资源触发和每日全员补漏处理。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import datetime\n"
    "from collections import Counter\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Set, Tuple\n"
    "\n"
    "from app.chain.media import MediaChain\n"
    "from app.schemas.types import MediaSource, MediaType\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaAiringWeeklyV1121Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"在 v1.12.0 逐集日历之上增加星期调度与周视图。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260903-r45-preview\"\n"
    "    _weekday_confidence_min_v1121 = 0.60\n"
    "    _weekday_sample_limit_v1121 = 16\n"
    "    _poster_cache_days_v1121 = 7\n"
    "    _weekday_labels_v1121 = (\"周一\", \"周二\", \"周三\", \"周四\", \"周五\", \"周六\", \"周日\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _date_v1121(value: Any) -> Optional[datetime.date]:\n"
    "        text = str(value or \"\").strip()[:10]\n"
    "        if not text:\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return datetime.date.fromisoformat(text)\n"
    "        except ValueError:\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _positive_set_v1121(values: Iterable[Any]) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        result: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        for raw in values or []:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                value = int(raw or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if value > 0:\n"
    "                result.add(value)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _weekly_pattern_v1121(self, item: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"从最近已知 air_date 推断稳定周更星期；样本不足或分布不稳定时不猜。\"\"\"\n"
    "        rows: List[Tuple[datetime.date, int]] = []\n"
    "        for row in (item or {}).get(\"episodes\") or []:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            day = self._date_v1121(row.get(\"air_date\"))\n"
    "            if not day:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                episode = int(row.get(\"episode\") or row.get(\"episode_number\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                episode = 0\n"
    "            if episode > 0:\n"
    "                rows.append((day, episode))\n"
    "        rows.sort(key=lambda pair: (pair[0], pair[1]))\n"
    "        rows = rows[-int(self._weekday_sample_limit_v1121):]\n"
    "        if len(rows) < 2:\n"
    "            return {\"weekday\": None, \"confidence\": 0.0, \"samples\": len(rows)}\n"
    "        counts = Counter(day.weekday() for day, _ in rows)\n"
    "        weekday, hits = max(counts.items(), key=lambda pair: (pair[1], pair[0]))\n"
    "        confidence = hits / max(len(rows), 1)\n"
    "        if hits < 2 or confidence < float(self._weekday_confidence_min_v1121):\n"
    "            return {\"weekday\": None, \"confidence\": round(confidence, 3), \"samples\": len(rows)}\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"weekday\": int(weekday),\n"
    "            \"weekday_label\": self._weekday_labels_v1121[int(weekday)],\n"
    "            \"confidence\": round(confidence, 3),\n"
    "            \"samples\": len(rows),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _scheduled_rows_v1121(self, item: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        result: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        for row in (item or {}).get(\"episodes\") or []:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                episode = int(row.get(\"episode\") or row.get(\"episode_number\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if episode > 0:\n"
    "                result[episode] = dict(row)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _airing_gate_v1120(self, subscribe: Any, payload: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"收紧 date 精度到自然日，并为缺日期周更剧增加星期级回退。\"\"\"\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._airing_gate_v1120(subscribe, payload=payload) or {})\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        calendar = payload or self._refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(force=False)\n"
    "        item = self._calendar_item_for_v1120(subscribe, calendar)\n"
    "        scheduled_rows = self._scheduled_rows_v1121(item)\n"
    "        today = datetime.date.today()\n"
    "\n"
    "        due = self._positive_set_v1121(result.get(\"due_missing\") or [])\n"
    "        future = self._positive_set_v1121(result.get(\"future_missing\") or [])\n"
    "        unscheduled = self._positive_set_v1121(result.get(\"unscheduled_missing\") or [])\n"
    "        reserved = self._positive_set_v1121(result.get(\"reserved\") or [])\n"
    "        claimed = self._positive_set_v1121(result.get(\"claimed\") or [])\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 只有日期时严格等到该自然日；精确 air_at 仍允许 v1.12.0 的提前窗口。\n"
    "        strict_removed: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        for episode in list(due):\n"
    "            row = scheduled_rows.get(episode) or {}\n"
    "            if str(row.get(\"precision\") or \"\") != \"date\":\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            air_date = self._date_v1121(row.get(\"air_date\"))\n"
    "            if air_date and air_date > today:\n"
    "                due.discard(episode)\n"
    "                future.add(episode)\n"
    "                strict_removed.add(episode)\n"
    "\n"
    "        pattern = self._weekly_pattern_v1121(item)\n"
    "        fallback_episode = 0\n"
    "        inferred_weekday = pattern.get(\"weekday\")\n"
    "        if (\n"
    "            not (due - reserved - claimed)\n"
    "            and unscheduled\n"
    "            and inferred_weekday is not None\n"
    "            and int(inferred_weekday) == today.weekday()\n"
    "        ):\n"
    "            fallback_episode = min(unscheduled)\n"
    "            due.add(fallback_episode)\n"
    "            unscheduled.discard(fallback_episode)\n"
    "\n"
    "        result.update({\n"
    "            \"due_missing\": sorted(due),\n"
    "            \"due_uncovered\": sorted(due - reserved - claimed),\n"
    "            \"future_missing\": sorted(future),\n"
    "            \"unscheduled_missing\": sorted(unscheduled),\n"
    "            \"weekday\": inferred_weekday,\n"
    "            \"weekday_label\": pattern.get(\"weekday_label\") or \"\",\n"
    "            \"weekday_confidence\": pattern.get(\"confidence\") or 0.0,\n"
    "            \"weekday_samples\": pattern.get(\"samples\") or 0,\n"
    "            \"weekday_fallback\": bool(fallback_episode),\n"
    "            \"weekday_fallback_episode\": fallback_episode,\n"
    "            \"date_strict_removed\": sorted(strict_removed),\n"
    "            \"date_strict\": True,\n"
    "        })\n"
    "\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        state = self.get_data(\"airing_gate_state_v1120\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(state, dict):\n"
    "            state = {}\n"
    "        state[str(sid)] = result\n"
    "        if len(state) > 1000:\n"
    "            state = dict(list(state.items())[-1000:])\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"airing_gate_state_v1120\", state)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if fallback_episode:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【星期排期】#%s %s 最近排期稳定在%s（置信 %.0f%%/%s集），TMDB 下一缺集无日期；今天命中星期，仅补 E%02d\",\n"
    "                sid,\n"
    "                str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                str(pattern.get(\"weekday_label\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                float(pattern.get(\"confidence\") or 0.0) * 100,\n"
    "                int(pattern.get(\"samples\") or 0),\n"
    "                fallback_episode,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _poster_v1121(self, item: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n"
    "        for key in (\"poster\", \"poster_url\", \"poster_path\"):\n"
    "            value = str(item.get(key) or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if value.startswith(\"http://\") or value.startswith(\"https://\"):\n"
    "                return value\n"
    "        tmdb_id = str(item.get(\"tmdb_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not tmdb_id.isdigit():\n"
    "            return \"\"\n"
    "        cache = self.get_data(\"airing_poster_cache_v1121\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(cache, dict):\n"
    "            cache = {}\n"
    "        entry = cache.get(tmdb_id) if isinstance(cache.get(tmdb_id), dict) else {}\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            fetched_at = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(str(entry.get(\"fetched_at\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            fetched_at = None\n"
    "        if fetched_at and datetime.datetime.now() - fetched_at < datetime.timedelta(days=self._poster_cache_days_v1121):\n"
    "            return str(entry.get(\"poster\") or \"\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        poster = \"\"\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                recognize = getattr(self, \"_recognize_media_cached_v208\", None)\n"
    "                if callable(recognize):\n"
    "                    info = recognize(\n"
    "                        mtype=MediaType.TV,\n"
    "                        media_source=MediaSource.TMDB,\n"
    "                        media_id=tmdb_id,\n"
    "                        cache=True,\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    info = MediaChain().recognize_media(\n"
    "                        mtype=MediaType.TV,\n"
    "                        media_source=MediaSource.TMDB,\n"
    "                        media_id=tmdb_id,\n"
    "                        cache=True,\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "            except TypeError:\n"
    "                recognize = getattr(self, \"_recognize_media_cached_v208\", None)\n"
    "                if callable(recognize):\n"
    "                    info = recognize(mtype=MediaType.TV, media_source=MediaSource.TMDB, media_id=tmdb_id)\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    info = MediaChain().recognize_media(mtype=MediaType.TV, media_source=MediaSource.TMDB, media_id=tmdb_id)\n"
    "            getter = getattr(info, \"get_poster_image\", None) if info else None\n"
    "            if callable(getter):\n"
    "                poster = str(getter() or \"\")\n"
    "            if not poster and info:\n"
    "                poster = str(getattr(info, \"poster\", None) or getattr(info, \"poster_path\", None) or \"\")\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            poster = \"\"\n"
    "        cache[tmdb_id] = {\"poster\": poster, \"fetched_at\": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")}\n"
    "        if len(cache) > 500:\n"
    "            cache = dict(list(cache.items())[-500:])\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"airing_poster_cache_v1121\", cache)\n"
    "        return poster\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _movie_pool_v1121(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        selected = {\n"
    "            int(value) for value in (getattr(self, \"_selected_subscriptions\", []) or [])\n"
    "            if str(value).isdigit() and int(value) > 0\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for subscribe in self._list_subscriptions(None) or []:\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if sid not in selected or not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if str(getattr(subscribe, \"state\", \"\") or \"\") not in {\"N\", \"R\"}:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            rows.append({\n"
    "                \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                \"title\": str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                \"year\": str(getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _weekly_calendar_snapshot_v1121(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        calendar = self._refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(force=False)\n"
    "        today = datetime.date.today()\n"
    "        week_start = today - datetime.timedelta(days=today.weekday())\n"
    "        week_end = week_start + datetime.timedelta(days=6)\n"
    "        days = [{\n"
    "            \"date\": (week_start + datetime.timedelta(days=index)).isoformat(),\n"
    "            \"weekday\": index,\n"
    "            \"weekday_label\": self._weekday_labels_v1121[index],\n"
    "            \"is_today\": index == today.weekday(),\n"
    "            \"items\": [],\n"
    "        } for index in range(7)]\n"
    "\n"
    "        library = pending = inflight = 0\n"
    "        for item in calendar.get(\"subscriptions\") or []:\n"
    "            if not isinstance(item, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            sid = int(item.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0)\n"
    "            subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid) if sid else None\n"
    "            if not subscribe:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            raw_missing = self._positive_set_v1121(self._raw_subscription_missing_v1120(subscribe))\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                reservations = dict(self._pending_reservations(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "                reserved = self._positive_set_v1121(reservations.get(\"episodes\") or [])\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                reserved = set()\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                claimed = self._positive_set_v1121(self._active_source_claims(sid) or [])\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                claimed = set()\n"
    "            poster = self._poster_v1121(item)\n"
    "            pattern = self._weekly_pattern_v1121(item)\n"
    "\n"
    "            for raw in item.get(\"episodes\") or []:\n"
    "                if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                day = self._date_v1121(raw.get(\"air_date\"))\n"
    "                if not day or day < week_start or day > week_end:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    episode = int(raw.get(\"episode\") or raw.get(\"episode_number\") or 0)\n"
    "                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                    episode = 0\n"
    "                if episode <= 0:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "\n"
    "                if day > today:\n"
    "                    status, status_label = \"scheduled\", \"待更新\"\n"
    "                elif episode in reserved or episode in claimed:\n"
    "                    status, status_label = \"inflight\", \"转存中\"\n"
    "                    inflight += 1\n"
    "                elif episode in raw_missing:\n"
    "                    status, status_label = \"pending\", \"待补\"\n"
    "                    pending += 1\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    status, status_label = \"library\", \"已入库\"\n"
    "                    library += 1\n"
    "\n"
    "                days[day.weekday()][\"items\"].append({\n"
    "                    \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                    \"title\": str(item.get(\"title\") or getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"year\": str(item.get(\"year\") or getattr(subscribe, \"year\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"tmdb_id\": str(item.get(\"tmdb_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"season\": int(item.get(\"season\") or getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0) or 1),\n"
    "                    \"episode\": episode,\n"
    "                    \"air_date\": day.isoformat(),\n"
    "                    \"air_at\": str(raw.get(\"air_at\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    \"precision\": str(raw.get(\"precision\") or \"date\"),\n"
    "                    \"poster\": poster,\n"
    "                    \"status\": status,\n"
    "                    \"status_label\": status_label,\n"
    "                    \"weekday_label\": self._weekday_labels_v1121[day.weekday()],\n"
    "                    \"series_weekday\": pattern.get(\"weekday_label\") or \"\",\n"
    "                    \"series_weekday_confidence\": pattern.get(\"confidence\") or 0.0,\n"
    "                })\n"
    "\n"
    "        for day in days:\n"
    "            day[\"items\"].sort(key=lambda row: (str(row.get(\"air_at\") or row.get(\"air_date\") or \"\"), str(row.get(\"title\") or \"\")))\n"
    "            day[\"count\"] = len(day[\"items\"])\n"
    "            day[\"library\"] = sum(1 for row in day[\"items\"] if row.get(\"status\") == \"library\")\n"
    "            day[\"pending\"] = sum(1 for row in day[\"items\"] if row.get(\"status\") == \"pending\")\n"
    "            day[\"inflight\"] = sum(1 for row in day[\"items\"] if row.get(\"status\") == \"inflight\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        movies = self._movie_pool_v1121()\n"
    "        snapshot = {\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n"
    "            \"week_start\": week_start.isoformat(),\n"
    "            \"week_end\": week_end.isoformat(),\n"
    "            \"today\": today.isoformat(),\n"
    "            \"today_index\": today.weekday(),\n"
    "            \"week_total\": sum(int(day.get(\"count\") or 0) for day in days),\n"
    "            \"today_total\": len(days[today.weekday()][\"items\"]),\n"
    "            \"library\": library,\n"
    "            \"pending\": pending,\n"
    "            \"inflight\": inflight,\n"
    "            \"days\": days,\n"
    "            \"movies\": movies,\n"
    "            \"movie_count\": len(movies),\n"
    "            \"calendar_provider_dailyassistant\": int(calendar.get(\"dailyassistant\") or 0),\n"
    "            \"calendar_provider_fallback\": int(calendar.get(\"fallback\") or 0),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"airing_week_view_v1121\", snapshot)\n"
    "        return snapshot\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _legacy_calendar_card_v1121(node: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "        if not isinstance(node, dict) or node.get(\"component\") != \"VCard\":\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        return any(\n"
    "            isinstance(child, dict)\n"
    "            and child.get(\"component\") == \"VCardTitle\"\n"
    "            and str(child.get(\"text\") or \"\") == \"追更日历与每日补漏\"\n"
    "            for child in node.get(\"content\") or []\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _metric_chip_v1121(label: str, value: Any, icon: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VChip\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\"variant\": \"tonal\", \"prependIcon\": icon, \"class\": \"ma-1\"},\n"
    "            \"text\": f\"{label} {value}\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _episode_card_v1121(self, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        status = str(row.get(\"status\") or \"\")\n"
    "        color = {\"library\": \"success\", \"pending\": \"error\", \"inflight\": \"warning\"}.get(status, \"info\")\n"
    "        poster = str(row.get(\"poster\") or \"\")\n"
    "        media = ({\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VImg\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\"src\": poster, \"height\": \"220\", \"cover\": True},\n"
    "        } if poster.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")) else {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VSheet\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\"height\": \"220\", \"class\": \"d-flex align-center justify-center\", \"color\": \"surface-variant\"},\n"
    "            \"content\": [{\"component\": \"VIcon\", \"props\": {\"icon\": \"mdi-television-classic\", \"size\": \"56\"}}],\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        season = int(row.get(\"season\") or 1)\n"
    "        episode = int(row.get(\"episode\") or 0)\n"
    "        air_at = str(row.get(\"air_at\") or \"\")\n"
    "        time_text = air_at[11:16] if \"T\" in air_at and len(air_at) >= 16 else \"日期排期\"\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\"variant\": \"outlined\", \"class\": \"h-100 overflow-hidden\"},\n"
    "            \"content\": [\n"
    "                media,\n"
    "                {\"component\": \"VCardText\", \"content\": [\n"
    "                    {\"component\": \"VChip\", \"props\": {\"size\": \"x-small\", \"variant\": \"flat\", \"color\": color, \"class\": \"mb-2\"}, \"text\": str(row.get(\"status_label\") or \"\")},\n"
    "                    {\"component\": \"div\", \"props\": {\"class\": \"text-subtitle-2 font-weight-bold text-truncate\"}, \"text\": str(row.get(\"title\") or \"-\")},\n"
    "                    {\"component\": \"div\", \"props\": {\"class\": \"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mt-1\"}, \"text\": f\"S{season:02d}E{episode:02d} · {time_text}\"},\n"
    "                ]},\n"
    "            ],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _weekly_page_v1121(self, snapshot: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        days = list(snapshot.get(\"days\") or [])\n"
    "        today_index = int(snapshot.get(\"today_index\") or 0)\n"
    "        today = days[today_index] if 0 <= today_index < len(days) else {\"items\": []}\n"
    "        day_strip: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for day in days:\n"
    "            title = f\"今天 · {day.get('weekday_label')}\" if day.get(\"is_today\") else str(day.get(\"weekday_label\") or \"\")\n"
    "            day_strip.append({\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VCol\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\"cols\": 6, \"sm\": 4, \"md\": 3, \"lg\": 2, \"style\": \"min-width:135px; flex:1 1 0;\"},\n"
    "                \"content\": [{\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\"variant\": \"tonal\" if day.get(\"is_today\") else \"outlined\", \"class\": \"h-100\"},\n"
    "                    \"content\": [{\"component\": \"VCardText\", \"content\": [\n"
    "                        {\"component\": \"div\", \"props\": {\"class\": \"text-subtitle-2 font-weight-bold\"}, \"text\": title},\n"
    "                        {\"component\": \"div\", \"props\": {\"class\": \"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\"}, \"text\": str(day.get(\"date\") or \"\")[5:]},\n"

    "                    ]}],\n"
    "                }],\n"
    "            })\n"
    "\n"

    "            \"content\": [self._episode_card_v1121(row)],\n"
    "        } for row in (today.get(\"items\") or [])]\n"
    "        if not today_cards:\n"

    "                \"component\": \"VCol\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\"cols\": 12},\n"
    "                \"content\": [{\"component\": \"VAlert\", \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"type\": \"info\", \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                    \"text\": \"今天没有进入排期的剧集；其它星期的订阅不会触发外部资源站搜索。\",\n"
    "                }}],\n"
    "            }]\n"
    "\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\"variant\": \"outlined\", \"class\": \"mb-3\"},\n"
    "            \"content\": [\n"
    "                {\"component\": \"VCardTitle\", \"text\": \"追剧日历\"},\n"
    "                {\"component\": \"VCardSubtitle\", \"text\": \"逐集上映日期 + 星期排期驱动光鸭搜索；电影不参与星期筛选。\"},\n"
    "                {\"component\": \"VCardText\", \"content\": [\n"
    "                    {\"component\": \"div\", \"props\": {\"class\": \"d-flex flex-wrap mb-3\"}, \"content\": [\n"
    "                        self._metric_chip_v1121(\"本周更新\", snapshot.get(\"week_total\") or 0, \"mdi-calendar-week\"),\n"
    "                        self._metric_chip_v1121(\"今日更新\", snapshot.get(\"today_total\") or 0, \"mdi-white-balance-sunny\"),\n"
    "                        self._metric_chip_v1121(\"已入库\", snapshot.get(\"library\") or 0, \"mdi-check-circle-outline\"),\n"
    "                        self._metric_chip_v1121(\"待补\", snapshot.get(\"pending\") or 0, \"mdi-clock-alert-outline\"),\n"
    "                        self._metric_chip_v1121(\"电影待匹配\", snapshot.get(\"movie_count\") or 0, \"mdi-movie-open-outline\"),\n"
    "                    ]},\n"
    "                    {\"component\": \"VRow\", \"props\": {\"dense\": True}, \"content\": day_strip},\n"
    "                    {\"component\": \"div\", \"props\": {\"class\": \"text-subtitle-1 font-weight-bold mt-4 mb-2\"}, \"text\": f\"今日更新 · {snapshot.get('today')} · {int(snapshot.get('today_total') or 0)} 部\"},\n"
    "                    {\"component\": \"VRow\", \"props\": {\"dense\": True}, \"content\": today_cards},\n"
    "                    {\"component\": \"VAlert\", \"props\": {\n"
    "                        \"type\": \"info\", \"variant\": \"tonal\", \"class\": \"mt-3\",\n"
    "                        \"text\": (\n"
    "                            \"星期筛选只约束剧集普通后台匹配：例如周四更新的《完美世界》只在周四进入日常搜索，\"\n"
    "                            \"周五更新的《沧元图》只在周五进入日常搜索。电影继续由新资源触发 + 每日全员补漏处理；\"\n"
    "                            \"人工强制和每日 04:10 全员补漏不会被星期门禁挡住。\"\n"
    "                        ),\n"
    "                    }},\n"
    "                ]},\n"
    "            ],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_page(self):\n"
    "        pages = [node for node in list(super().get_page() or []) if not self._legacy_calendar_card_v1121(node)]\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            weekly = self._weekly_page_v1121(self._weekly_calendar_snapshot_v1121())\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【追剧日历】周视图生成失败：%s\", err)\n"
    "            weekly = {\"component\": \"VAlert\", \"props\": {\n"
    "                \"type\": \"warning\", \"variant\": \"tonal\", \"class\": \"mb-3\",\n"
    "                \"text\": f\"追剧日历暂时无法生成：{str(err)[:220]}\",\n"
    "            }}\n"
    "        return [weekly, *pages]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaAiringWeeklyV1121Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

    "            params = inspect.signature(fn).parameters\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"

    "        return any(p.kind == inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD for p in params.values())\n"

    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # Authoritative missing via SubscribeChain.resolve_subscribe_missing\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _mp_authoritative_missing_episodes_v209(self, subscribe: Any) -> Tuple[str, Set[int]]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"Return (source, missing_episodes).\n"
    "\n"
    "        source meanings:\n"
    "        - used_complete: MP positively reports coverage / exist_flag\n"
    "        - used_missing: MP returned an explicit non-empty missing list\n"
    "        - used_empty: MP returned NotExistMediaInfo-style empty episodes (NOT satisfied)\n"
    "        - fallback: library/logical intersection fallback\n"
    "        - movie: movie subscription\n"
    "        \"\"\"\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return \"movie\", set()\n"
    "        # Prefer MP resolve_subscribe_missing when available.\n"
    "        if self._mp_has_resolve_missing_v209():\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                from app.chain.subscribe import SubscribeChain\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    from app.application.subscription.contract import build_subscribe_meta\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    from app.chain.subscribe import build_subscribe_meta  # type: ignore\n"
    "\n"
    "                meta = build_subscribe_meta(subscribe)\n"
    "                recognize = getattr(self, \"_recognize_media_cached_v208\", None)\n"
    "                kwargs = {\n"
    "                    \"meta\": meta,\n"
    "                    \"mtype\": getattr(meta, \"type\", None),\n"
    "                    \"media_source\": getattr(subscribe, \"media_source\", None),\n"
    "                    \"media_id\": getattr(subscribe, \"media_id\", None),\n"
    "                    \"cache\": False,\n"
    "                }\n"
    "                if self._callable_accepts_kw_v209(recognize or (lambda **k: None), \"episode_group\"):\n"
    "                    kwargs[\"episode_group\"] = getattr(subscribe, \"episode_group\", None)\n"
    "                if callable(recognize):\n"
    "                    mediainfo = recognize(**kwargs)\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    from app.chain.media import MediaChain\n"
    "                    mediainfo = MediaChain().recognize_media(**kwargs)\n"
    "                if mediainfo is not None:\n"
    "                    chain = SubscribeChain()\n"
    "                    method = chain.resolve_subscribe_missing\n"
    "                    call_kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {\n"
    "                        \"subscribe\": subscribe,\n"
    "                        \"meta\": meta,\n"
    "                        \"mediainfo\": mediainfo,\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "                    if self._callable_accepts_kw_v209(method, \"best_version_accept_downloaded\"):\n"
    "                        call_kwargs[\"best_version_accept_downloaded\"] = False\n"
    "                    exist_flag, no_exists = method(**call_kwargs)\n"
    "                    if exist_flag:\n"
    "                        return \"used_complete\", set()\n"
    "                    season = int(getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "                    missing: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "                    saw_season_detail = False\n"
    "                    total_episode_hint = 0\n"
    "                    for season_map in (no_exists or {}).values():\n"
    "                        if not isinstance(season_map, dict):\n"
    "                            continue\n"
    "                        detail = season_map.get(season)\n"
    "                        if detail is None:\n"
    "                            detail = season_map.get(str(season))\n"
    "                        if detail is None:\n"
    "                            continue\n"
    "                        saw_season_detail = True\n"
    "                        episodes = getattr(detail, \"episodes\", None)\n"
    "                        if episodes is None and isinstance(detail, dict):\n"
    "                            episodes = detail.get(\"episodes\")\n"
    "                        try:\n"
    "                            total_episode_hint = int(\n"
    "                                getattr(detail, \"total_episode\", None)\n"
    "                                or (detail.get(\"total_episode\") if isinstance(detail, dict) else 0)\n"
    "                                or 0\n"
    "                            )\n"
    "                        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                            total_episode_hint = 0\n"
    "                        for value in episodes or []:\n"
    "                            try:\n"
    "                                episode = int(value)\n"
    "                            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                                continue\n"
    "                            if episode > 0:\n"
    "                                missing.add(episode)\n"
    "                    if missing:\n"
    "                        return \"used_missing\", missing\n"
    "                    # Empty episode list with a season detail / total hint is UNKNOWN, not complete.\n"
    "                    if saw_season_detail:\n"
    "                        try:\n"
    "                            sub_total = int(getattr(subscribe, \"total_episode\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "                        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                            sub_total = 0\n"
    "                        if total_episode_hint > 0 or sub_total > 0 or season > 0:\n"
    "                            return \"used_empty\", set()\n"
    "                    # No usable season detail → treat as empty/unknown rather than complete.\n"
    "                    return \"used_empty\", set()\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【MP订阅日历】resolve_subscribe_missing 不可用，回退媒体库同步：%s\",\n"
    "                    str(err)[:220],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "\n"
    "        # Fallback: prefer Emby/library missing. Logical/note missing is auxiliary only —\n"
    "        # never hard-cap Emby gaps away via intersection.\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            ctx = None\n"
    "            getter = getattr(self, \"_episode_run_context_for_subscribe_v211\", None)\n"
    "            if callable(getter):\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    ctx = getter(subscribe)\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    ctx = None\n"
    "            if isinstance(ctx, dict) and isinstance(ctx.get(\"library_sync\"), dict):\n"
    "                sync = dict(ctx.get(\"library_sync\") or {})\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                before = int(ctx.get(\"emby_query_count\") or 0) if isinstance(ctx, dict) else 0\n"
    "                sync = dict(self._sync_media_library_progress(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "                if isinstance(ctx, dict) and int(ctx.get(\"emby_query_count\") or 0) > before:\n"
    "                    ctx[\"mp_missing_related_library_calls\"] = int(\n"
    "                        ctx.get(\"mp_missing_related_library_calls\") or 0\n"
    "                    ) + 1\n"
    "            if not bool(sync.get(\"success\")):\n"
    "                library_missing = set()\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                library_missing = {\n"
    "                    int(v) for v in (sync.get(\"missing\") or []) if int(v or 0) > 0\n"
    "                }\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            library_missing = set()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            logical = set(self._raw_subscription_missing_v1120(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            logical = set()\n"
    "        if library_missing:\n"
    "            return \"fallback\", set(library_missing)\n"
    "        if logical:\n"
    "            return \"fallback\", set(logical)\n"
    "        return \"fallback\", set()\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # MP Native calendar provider (same identity + episode_group as cache_calendar)\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _mp_native_calendar_for_request_v209(self, request: Dict[str, Any], force: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        sid = int(request.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0)\n"
    "        subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid) if sid else None\n"
    "        season = int(request.get(\"season\") or getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 1) or 1)\n"
    "        episode_group = getattr(subscribe, \"episode_group\", None) if subscribe is not None else request.get(\"episode_group\")\n"
    "        tmdb_id = str(request.get(\"tmdb_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        row = {\n"
    "            **request,\n"
    "            \"season\": season,\n"
    "            \"episode_group\": episode_group,\n"
    "            \"provider\": \"moviepilot_native\",\n"
    "            \"episodes\": [],\n"
    "            \"error\": \"\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if not self._mp_has_tmdb_episodes_v209():\n"
    "            row[\"error\"] = \"tmdb_episodes_unavailable\"\n"
    "            row[\"provider\"] = \"unavailable\"\n"
    "            return row\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            from app.chain.tmdb import TmdbChain\n"
    "            from app.chain.media import MediaChain\n"
    "            from app.schemas.types import MediaSource, MediaType\n"
    "\n"
    "            recognize = getattr(self, \"_recognize_media_cached_v208\", None)\n"
    "            recognize_kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {\n"
    "                \"mtype\": MediaType.TV,\n"
    "                \"media_source\": getattr(subscribe, \"media_source\", None) or MediaSource.TMDB,\n"
    "                \"media_id\": getattr(subscribe, \"media_id\", None) or tmdb_id,\n"
    "                \"cache\": not force,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            if callable(recognize):\n"
    "                target = recognize\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                target = MediaChain().recognize_media\n"
    "            if self._callable_accepts_kw_v209(target, \"episode_group\"):\n"
    "                recognize_kwargs[\"episode_group\"] = episode_group\n"
