    "  频道 Push / Airing Pull / 新订阅时按真实来源重新分组，不能被第一个 worker trigger 串改执行模式；\n"
    "- trigger 记录与 Governance/可靠性队列共用同一 RLock 完成原子入队，避免“刚判断未 active，下一瞬\n"
    "  被其它线程抢先 active”后留下陈旧 trigger 污染下一轮；\n"
    "- 频道故障恢复只恢复并消费频道，不得借恢复任务偷偷启动主动 GYING。\n"
    "\n"
    "本层是标准 cooperative mixin，不继承预览策略类；运行时由显式 MRO 把它放在\n"
    "GuangYaDispatchPolicyV1125Mixin 之前，所有 super() 都沿最终插件 MRO 继续下传。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import datetime\n"
    "import threading\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaDispatchPolicyFinalV1125Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"最终发布前调度权威。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260904-r51\"\n"
    "    _calendar_failure_backoff_seconds_v1125 = 60\n"
    "    _async_trigger_bucket_limit_v1125 = 8\n"
    "    _hourly_due_cooldown_seconds_v1125 = 60 * 60\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        self._dispatch_final_local_v1125 = threading.local()\n"
    "        self._dispatch_trigger_lock_v1125 = threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._dispatch_triggers_v1125: Dict[int, List[str]] = {}\n"
    "        self._calendar_refresh_failure_until_v1125 = 0.0\n"
    "        self._calendar_refresh_failure_message_v1125 = \"\"\n"
    "        return super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _airing_gate_v1120(self, subscribe: Any, payload: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        result = dict(super()._airing_gate_v1120(subscribe, payload=payload) or {})\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe) or bool(result.get(\"passive_channel_bypass_v1125\")):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        # Weekly 层只有在历史样本达到置信门槛时才会给出 weekday。\n"
    "        # 这已经足够说明“调度可判定”：今天命中则 due_uncovered 有值，非更新日则必须等待。\n"
    "        # 不能因为当前缺集没有逐集 air_date 就退回 legacy 全量缺集搜索。\n"
    "        if not bool(result.get(\"calendar_available\")) and result.get(\"weekday\") is not None:\n"
    "            result[\"calendar_explicit_available_v1125\"] = False\n"
    "            result[\"calendar_available\"] = True\n"
    "            result[\"calendar_available_basis_v1125\"] = \"stable_weekday\"\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _external_cooldown_due_v1125(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        sid: int,\n"
    "        state: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        now: float,\n"
    "    ) -> bool:\n"
    "        \"\"\"AiringDue 每小时只允许同一媒体进入一次主动资源复查窗口。\"\"\"\n"
    "        row = dict(state.get(str(sid)) or {})\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            last_at = float(row.get(\"last_at\") or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            last_at = 0.0\n"
    "        cooldown = max(60, int(getattr(self, \"_hourly_due_cooldown_seconds_v1125\", 60 * 60) or 60 * 60))\n"
    "        return not last_at or now - last_at >= cooldown\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _record_auto_external_cooldown_v1125(self, subscribe: Any, origin: str) -> None:\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        if sid <= 0:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            state = dict(self._external_search_state_v1114() or {})\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            state = {}\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        row = dict(state.get(str(sid)) or {})\n"
    "        row.update({\n"
    "            \"last_at\": now,\n"
    "            \"last_time\": self._now_text(),\n"
    "            \"cooldown_minutes\": int(getattr(self, \"_external_search_cooldown_minutes_v1114\", 180) or 180),\n"
    "            \"origin\": str(origin or \"automatic_force\"),\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        state[str(sid)] = row\n"
    "        if len(state) > 1000:\n"
    "            state = dict(sorted(\n"
    "                state.items(),\n"
    "                key=lambda pair: float((pair[1] or {}).get(\"last_at\") or 0),\n"
    "                reverse=True,\n"
    "            )[:1000])\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"external_search_guard\", state)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _claim_external_search_round_v1114(self, subscribe: Any, force: bool = False) -> bool:\n"
    "        reader = getattr(self, \"_route_source_mode_value_v1115\", None)\n"
    "        mode = str(reader() if callable(reader) else getattr(self, \"_route_source_mode_v1115\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        # AiringDue 已经由逐集日历/星期门禁证明“今天应该处理”。此时不能再套默认 180 分钟\n"
    "        # external_search_guard，否则“每小时检查”实际会变成三小时才访问一次 GYING/迅雷。\n"
    "        # 这里只把该模式收敛到 60 分钟；频道、人工、普通后台以及每日修复继续沿用原 Governance。\n"
    "        if not force and mode == \"airing_pull\":\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if sid <= 0:\n"
    "                return False\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                state = dict(self._external_search_state_v1114() or {})\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                state = {}\n"
    "            row = dict(state.get(str(sid)) or {})\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                last_at = float(row.get(\"last_at\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                last_at = 0.0\n"
    "            now = time.time()\n"
    "            cooldown = max(60, int(getattr(self, \"_hourly_due_cooldown_seconds_v1125\", 60 * 60) or 60 * 60))\n"
    "            allowed = not last_at or now - last_at >= cooldown\n"
    "            self._external_round_allowed_v1114[sid] = allowed\n"
    "            if allowed:\n"
    "                state[str(sid)] = {\n"
    "                    **row,\n"
    "                    \"last_at\": now,\n"
    "                    \"last_time\": self._now_text(),\n"
    "                    \"cooldown_minutes\": max(1, int(cooldown / 60)),\n"
    "                    \"origin\": \"airing_full_chain_v1125\",\n"
    "                }\n"
    "                if len(state) > 1000:\n"
    "                    state = dict(sorted(\n"
    "                        state.items(),\n"
    "                        key=lambda pair: float((pair[1] or {}).get(\"last_at\") or 0),\n"
    "                        reverse=True,\n"
    "                    )[:1000])\n"
    "                self.save_data(\"external_search_guard\", state)\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                remaining = max(1, int((cooldown - (now - last_at)) / 60))\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【今日资源复查】#%s %s 本小时已执行过完整资源链，约 %s 分钟后再复查\",\n"
    "                    sid,\n"
    "                    str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                    remaining,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            return allowed\n"
    "\n"
    "        allowed = bool(super()._claim_external_search_round_v1114(subscribe, force=force))\n"
    "        if not allowed or not force:\n"
    "            return allowed\n"
    "        if mode == \"daily_repair_pull\":\n"
    "            self._record_auto_external_cooldown_v1125(subscribe, \"daily_repair_pull\")\n"
    "        return allowed\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _spawn_route_prime(self, sids: Iterable[int], trigger: str = \"立即检查\") -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"新增订阅只做一次频道补查；主动站点搜索仍交给最终日历 selector。\"\"\"\n"
    "        ids = sorted(self._positive_ids_v1125(sids or []))\n"
    "        if not ids or not self._enabled:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "\n"
    "        missing_cache: List[int] = []\n"
    "        for sid in ids:\n"
    "            subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "            if not subscribe or not self._is_guangya_route(subscribe):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                if not self._cached_matches_for_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                    missing_cache.append(sid)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                missing_cache.append(sid)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if missing_cache:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【新订阅】%s 个订阅频道缓存未命中，合并现查频道一次；随后仅按更新日历判断是否主动搜索\",\n"
    "                len(missing_cache),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                self.refresh_channels(force=True)\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【新订阅】频道现查失败，仍继续按当前更新日历判断主动搜索：%s\",\n"
    "                    str(err)[:260],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【新订阅】进入频道缓存/现查 + 当前应播主动搜索判定，共 %s 个订阅\",\n"
    "            len(ids),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._queue_async_route_check(ids, trigger=\"新订阅资源匹配\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 异步队列来源保持：Reliability 合并 ID，但不能合并掉每个 ID 的来源语义。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _queue_async_route_check(self, sids: Iterable[int], trigger: str = \"后台检查\") -> None:\n"
    "        ids = sorted(self._positive_ids_v1125(sids or []))\n"
    "        if not ids or not bool(getattr(self, \"_enabled\", False)):\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        current = getattr(self, \"_runtime_is_current\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(current) and not bool(current()):\n"
    "            return\n"
    "\n"
    "        # _async_route_lock 在 Experience 初始化为 RLock。整个“看 active -> 记录 trigger ->\n"
    "        # Governance 再过滤 -> Reliability 入 pending/recheck”都在同一把锁里完成；后续 super\n"
    "        # 会可重入地拿同一 RLock，因此不会在预测与真正入队之间被 worker 改写 active。\n"
    "        route_lock = getattr(self, \"_async_route_lock\", None)\n"
    "        if route_lock is None:\n"
    "            route_lock = threading.RLock()\n"
    "            self._async_route_lock = route_lock\n"
    "\n"
    "        with route_lock:\n"
    "            accepted = set(ids)\n"
    "            automatic = getattr(self, \"_automatic_trigger_v1114\", None)\n"
    "            manual = getattr(self, \"_manual_trigger_v1114\", None)\n"
    "            is_automatic = bool(automatic(trigger)) if callable(automatic) else False\n"
    "            is_manual = bool(manual(trigger)) if callable(manual) else False\n"
    "            if is_automatic and not is_manual:\n"
    "                active = set(getattr(self, \"_async_route_active\", set()) or set())\n"
    "                accepted -= active\n"
    "\n"
    "            if accepted:\n"
    "                lock = getattr(self, \"_dispatch_trigger_lock_v1125\", None)\n"
    "                if lock is None:\n"
    "                    lock = threading.RLock()\n"
    "                    self._dispatch_trigger_lock_v1125 = lock\n"
    "                store = getattr(self, \"_dispatch_triggers_v1125\", None)\n"
    "                if not isinstance(store, dict):\n"
    "                    store = {}\n"
    "                    self._dispatch_triggers_v1125 = store\n"
    "                text = str(trigger or \"后台检查\")\n"
    "                with lock:\n"
    "                    for sid in sorted(accepted):\n"
    "                        bucket = list(store.get(sid) or [])\n"
    "                        # Reliability finally 的“后台合并补偿”只是内部重拉起标识；真实来源还在桶中时\n"
    "                        # 绝不能覆盖成 generic trigger。\n"
    "                        if text == \"后台合并补偿\" and bucket:\n"
    "                            continue\n"
    "                        if text not in bucket:\n"
    "                            bucket.append(text)\n"
    "                        limit = max(2, int(getattr(self, \"_async_trigger_bucket_limit_v1125\", 8) or 8))\n"
    "                        store[sid] = bucket[-limit:]\n"
    "\n"
    "            return super()._queue_async_route_check(ids, trigger=trigger)\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _ordered_async_triggers_v1125(values: Iterable[str], fallback: str) -> List[str]:\n"
    "        rows: List[str] = []\n"
    "        for raw in values or []:\n"
    "            value = str(raw or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if value and value not in rows:\n"
    "                rows.append(value)\n"
    "        if not rows:\n"
    "            rows = [str(fallback or \"后台检查\")]\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 新订阅任务自己已经包含“频道 -> 当前应播 Pull”，同一 debounce 内的普通频道/日历\n"
    "        # 自动触发无需再重复；显式人工操作保留。\n"
    "        prime = next((value for value in rows if \"新订阅资源匹配\" in value), \"\")\n"
    "        if prime:\n"
    "            manual_rows = [\n"
    "                value for value in rows\n"
    "                if any(token in value.lower() for token in (\"手动\", \"人工\", \"立即\", \"api\", \"控制台\", \"按钮\"))\n"
    "            ]\n"
    "            return [prime, *[value for value in manual_rows if value != prime]]\n"
    "\n"
    "        recovery = next((value for value in rows if \"频道故障自动恢复\" in value), \"\")\n"
    "        channel = [value for value in rows if \"频道新增资源\" in value]\n"
    "        active = [\n"
    "            value for value in rows\n"
    "            if \"观影定时轮询\" in value or \"更新日历\" in value or \"airing\" in value.lower()\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        others = [value for value in rows if value not in channel and value not in active and value != recovery]\n"
    "        ordered: List[str] = []\n"
    "        # 被动资源先消费，再允许主动 Pull 重算真实缺口。\n"
    "        if recovery:\n"
    "            ordered.append(recovery)\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            ordered.extend(channel)\n"
    "        ordered.extend(active)\n"
    "        ordered.extend(others)\n"
    "        return ordered or [str(fallback or \"后台检查\")]\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _take_async_route_triggers_v1125(self, batch: Iterable[int], fallback: str) -> Dict[int, List[str]]:\n"
    "        ids = sorted(self._positive_ids_v1125(batch or []))\n"
    "        store = getattr(self, \"_dispatch_triggers_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_dispatch_trigger_lock_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(store, dict) or lock is None:\n"
    "            return {sid: [str(fallback or \"后台检查\")] for sid in ids}\n"
    "        result: Dict[int, List[str]] = {}\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            for sid in ids:\n"
    "                values = list(store.pop(sid, []) or [])\n"
    "                result[sid] = self._ordered_async_triggers_v1125(values, fallback)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _async_trigger_priority_v1125(trigger: str) -> int:\n"
    "        text = str(trigger or \"\")\n"
    "        if \"频道故障自动恢复\" in text or \"频道新增资源\" in text:\n"
    "            return 0\n"
    "        if \"新订阅资源匹配\" in text or \"外部补搜\" in text:\n"
    "            return 1\n"
    "        if \"观影定时轮询\" in text or \"更新日历\" in text or \"airing\" in text.lower():\n"
    "            return 2\n"
    "        return 3\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _run_dispatch_trigger_v1125(self, ids: List[int], trigger: str) -> None:\n"
    "        text = str(trigger or \"\")\n"
    "        if not ids:\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "\n"
    "        if \"频道故障自动恢复\" in text:\n"
    "            # 这是频道 transport 的恢复任务，不是主动资源站调度器。恢复成功后只消费频道缓存；\n"
    "            # 常规 GYING 仍等 AiringDue/日历 selector。\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                self.refresh_channels(force=True)\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【频道恢复】恢复刷新仍失败，继续仅使用频道缓存，不启动 GYING：%s\",\n"
    "                    str(err)[:260],\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            return self._run_v1115_mode_batch(ids, text, \"channel_event\", force=False)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if \"外部补搜\" in text:\n"
    "            return self._run_v1115_mode_batch(ids, text, \"airing_pull\", force=True)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if \"新订阅资源匹配\" in text:\n"
    "            self._run_v1115_mode_batch(\n"
    "                ids,\n"
    "                \"新订阅资源匹配·频道阶段\",\n"
    "                \"channel_event\",\n"
    "                force=False,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            remaining: List[int] = []\n"
    "            checker = getattr(self, \"_manual_remaining_ids_v11211\", None)\n"
    "            if callable(checker):\n"
    "                remaining = list(checker(ids) or [])\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                remaining = list(ids)\n"
    "            if remaining:\n"
    "                self._run_v1115_mode_batch(\n"
    "                    remaining,\n"
    "                    \"新订阅资源匹配·外部补搜\",\n"
    "                    \"airing_pull\",\n"
    "                    force=True,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "\n"
    "        return super()._run_reliability_route_batch(ids, text)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _run_reliability_route_batch(self, batch: List[int], trigger: str) -> None:\n"
    "        ids = sorted(self._positive_ids_v1125(batch or []))\n"
    "        if not ids:\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        trigger_map = self._take_async_route_triggers_v1125(ids, trigger)\n"
    "        groups: Dict[str, List[int]] = {}\n"
    "        for sid in ids:\n"
    "            for value in trigger_map.get(sid) or [str(trigger or \"后台检查\")]:\n"
    "                groups.setdefault(str(value or \"后台检查\"), []).append(sid)\n"
    "\n"
    "        for value in sorted(groups, key=lambda item: (self._async_trigger_priority_v1125(item), item)):\n"
    "            group_ids = sorted(set(groups.get(value) or []))\n"
    "            self._run_dispatch_trigger_v1125(group_ids, value)\n"
    "        return None\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _calendar_failure_payload_v1125(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"必须保持 truthy，避免下层 `payload or refresh()` 再次发起网络请求。\"\"\"\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n"
    "            \"subscriptions\": [],\n"
    "            \"count\": 0,\n"
    "            \"calendar_refresh_failed_v1125\": True,\n"
    "            \"errors\": [str(getattr(self, \"_calendar_refresh_failure_message_v1125\", \"\") or \"calendar unavailable\")[:240]],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(self, force: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"每日阶段支持延迟强刷；普通刷新异常进入短退避，防止每订阅重复打日历服务。\"\"\"\n"
    "        local = getattr(self, \"_dispatch_final_local_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        if (\n"
    "            force\n"
    "            and local is not None\n"
    "            and bool(getattr(local, \"defer_daily_calendar\", False))\n"
    "        ):\n"
    "            cached = self.get_data(\"airing_calendar_v1120\") or {}\n"
    "            if isinstance(cached, dict) and cached:\n"
    "                return dict(cached)\n"
    "            return self._calendar_failure_payload_v1125()\n"
    "\n"
    "        if force:\n"
    "            return dict(super()._refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(force=True) or {})\n"
    "\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            failure_until = float(getattr(self, \"_calendar_refresh_failure_until_v1125\", 0.0) or 0.0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            failure_until = 0.0\n"
    "        if failure_until > now:\n"
    "            return self._calendar_failure_payload_v1125()\n"
    "\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            result = dict(super()._refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(force=False) or {})\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._calendar_refresh_failure_until_v1125 = now + max(\n"
    "                15,\n"
    "                int(getattr(self, \"_calendar_failure_backoff_seconds_v1125\", 60) or 60),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self._calendar_refresh_failure_message_v1125 = str(err)[:240]\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【更新日历】常规刷新异常，进入短退避避免按订阅重复请求：%s\",\n"
    "                str(err)[:240],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return self._calendar_failure_payload_v1125()\n"
    "\n"
    "        self._calendar_refresh_failure_until_v1125 = 0.0\n"
    "        self._calendar_refresh_failure_message_v1125 = \"\"\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _daily_full_catchup_v1110(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"严格执行频道 -> 剩余 GYING -> 日历刷新，不让日历网络请求挡在频道前。\"\"\"\n"
    "        local = getattr(self, \"_dispatch_final_local_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        if local is None:\n"
    "            local = threading.local()\n"
    "            self._dispatch_final_local_v1125 = local\n"
    "        previous = bool(getattr(local, \"defer_daily_calendar\", False))\n"
    "        local.defer_daily_calendar = True\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            result = super()._daily_full_catchup_v1110()\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            local.defer_daily_calendar = previous\n"
    "\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self._refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(force=True)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【每日全员复核】两阶段补漏已完成，但末尾更新日历刷新失败：%s\",\n"
    "                str(err)[:260],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaDispatchPolicyFinalV1125Mixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== dispatch_policy_v1125.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["dispatch_policy_v1125"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.12.5：频道 Push / 观影 Pull 调度收口层。\n"
    "\n"
    "原则：\n"
    "- 5 分钟 tick 只负责频道增量，不再同时启动主动 GYING；\n"
    "- 频道是被动到达的资源，命中真实缺集后不受播出日期门禁限制，也不依赖日历服务可用，\n"
    "  但仍受媒体身份、reservation/source claim、episode fence 与质量门禁限制；频道批次继续禁止主动 GYING；\n"
    "- 主动 GYING 只由更新日历服务统一驱动：TV/动漫先按 due_uncovered 过滤，电影按外部\n"
    "  检索冷却参与；日历不可用时才退回旧的“真实缺集 + 冷却”语义；\n"
    "- 每天 04:10 全员复核改为两阶段：先用频道缓存/现查补全，再只对仍未覆盖且不在途的\n"
    "  订阅做一次强制 GYING 补漏，避免频道已有资源时仍先打观影服务器；\n"
    "- due scope 改为线程局部上下文，避免频道、日历、人工任务并发处理不同订阅时互相串集数范围。\n"
    "\n"
    "这个层只改“什么时候触发哪个来源”和调度上下文隔离，不改变迅雷 JSON、媒体身份、资源质量、\n"
    "Episode Resolver、光鸭转存/cloudcollection 与 MoviePilot 完成事实的既有实现。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import datetime\n"
    "import threading\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from contextlib import contextmanager\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Set, Tuple\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaDispatchPolicyV1125Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"最终触发策略：频道被动消费、日历主动拉取、每日两阶段修复。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260904-r51\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        # 两类线程局部状态分别保护：\n"
    "        # 1) 5 分钟 tick 只抑制当前 tick 线程的旧 viewing poll；\n"
    "        # 2) due scope 只约束当前来源执行线程，不能串到另一个订阅线程。\n"
    "        self._dispatch_tick_local_v1125 = threading.local()\n"
    "        self._airing_scope_local_v1125 = threading.local()\n"
    "        return super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _positive_ids_v1125(values: Iterable[Any]) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        result: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "        for raw in values or []:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                value = int(raw or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if value > 0:\n"
    "                result.add(value)\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # due scope 并发隔离\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _airing_scope_local_value_v1125(self):\n"
    "        local = getattr(self, \"_airing_scope_local_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        if local is None:\n"
    "            local = threading.local()\n"
    "            self._airing_scope_local_v1125 = local\n"
    "        return local\n"
    "\n"
    "    @contextmanager\n"
    "    def _due_scope_v1120(self, subscribe: Any, episodes: Iterable[int]):\n"
    "        local = self._airing_scope_local_value_v1125()\n"
    "        had_scope = hasattr(local, \"scope\")\n"
    "        previous = getattr(local, \"scope\", None)\n"
    "        local.scope = {\n"
    "            \"subscribe_id\": int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0),\n"
    "            \"episodes\": sorted(self._positive_ids_v1125(episodes)),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            yield\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            if had_scope:\n"
    "                local.scope = previous\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    delattr(local, \"scope\")\n"
    "                except AttributeError:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "\n"
    "    @contextmanager\n"
    "    def _without_due_scope_v1120(self):\n"
    "        local = self._airing_scope_local_value_v1125()\n"
    "        had_scope = hasattr(local, \"scope\")\n"
    "        previous = getattr(local, \"scope\", None)\n"
    "        local.scope = None\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            yield\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            if had_scope:\n"
    "                local.scope = previous\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    delattr(local, \"scope\")\n"
    "                except AttributeError:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _subscription_missing_episodes(self, subscribe: Any) -> List[int]:\n"
    "        # 下层 scheduler 的历史共享 scope 在最终运行时始终保持 None；最终裁剪只认本层 thread-local。\n"
    "        values = sorted(self._positive_ids_v1125(super()._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or []))\n"
    "        local = self._airing_scope_local_value_v1125()\n"
    "        scope = getattr(local, \"scope\", None)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(scope, dict):\n"
    "            return values\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        if sid != int(scope.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0):\n"
    "            return values\n"
    "        allowed = self._positive_ids_v1125(scope.get(\"episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        return [value for value in values if value in allowed]\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _active_selected_subscriptions_v1125(self) -> List[Any]:\n"
    "        selected = self._positive_ids_v1125(getattr(self, \"_selected_subscriptions\", []) or [])\n"
    "        rows: List[Any] = []\n"
    "        for subscribe in self._list_subscriptions(\"N,R\") or []:\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if sid <= 0 or sid not in selected:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                if not self._is_guangya_route(subscribe):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            rows.append(subscribe)\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _uncovered_missing_v1125(self, subscribe: Any) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return set()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            with self._without_due_scope_v1120():\n"
    "                missing = self._positive_ids_v1125(self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            missing = set()\n"
    "        if not missing:\n"
    "            return set()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            reservations = dict(self._pending_reservations(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "            reserved = self._positive_ids_v1125(reservations.get(\"episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            reserved = set()\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            claimed = self._positive_ids_v1125(self._active_source_claims(sid) or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            claimed = set()\n"
    "        return missing - reserved - claimed\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _movie_needs_pull_v1125(self, subscribe: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "        if not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        confirmed = getattr(self, \"_movie_transfer_confirmed\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(confirmed):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                if bool(confirmed(subscribe)):\n"
    "                    try:\n"
    "                        self._finish_subscription_if_complete(subscribe)\n"
    "                    except Exception:\n"
    "                        pass\n"
    "                    return False\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            reservations = dict(self._pending_reservations(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "            if bool(reservations.get(\"movie\")):\n"
    "                return False\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        return str(getattr(subscribe, \"state\", \"\") or \"\") in {\"N\", \"R\", \"\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 频道 Push：日期只控制主动搜索；被动资源完全不依赖日历服务。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _airing_gate_v1120(self, subscribe: Any, payload: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return dict(super()._airing_gate_v1120(subscribe, payload=payload) or {})\n"
    "\n"
    "        mode_reader = getattr(self, \"_route_source_mode_value_v1115\", None)\n"
    "        mode = str(mode_reader() if callable(mode_reader) else getattr(self, \"_route_source_mode_v1115\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if mode != \"channel_event\":\n"
    "            return dict(super()._airing_gate_v1120(subscribe, payload=payload) or {})\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 被动频道资源已经到达，本轮只需要 MoviePilot 真实缺集与在途事实；\n"
    "        # 不调用 super 的日历门禁，DailyAssistant/TMDB 故障也不能阻塞已到达资源。\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            with self._without_due_scope_v1120():\n"
    "                raw_missing = self._positive_ids_v1125(self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            raw_missing = set()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            reservations = dict(self._pending_reservations(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "            reserved = self._positive_ids_v1125(reservations.get(\"episodes\") or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            reserved = set()\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            claimed = self._positive_ids_v1125(self._active_source_claims(sid) or [])\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            claimed = set()\n"
    "        reserved &= raw_missing\n"
    "        claimed &= raw_missing\n"
    "        passive_uncovered = raw_missing - reserved - claimed\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 仅把最近一次严格日历快照作为诊断信息，不让它参与频道准入。\n"
    "        strict_state = self.get_data(\"airing_gate_state_v1120\") or {}\n"
    "        strict_row = dict(strict_state.get(str(sid)) or {}) if isinstance(strict_state, dict) else {}\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "            \"name\": str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"calendar_available\": True,\n"
    "            \"calendar_provider\": str(strict_row.get(\"calendar_provider\") or \"passive_channel\"),\n"
    "            \"raw_missing\": sorted(raw_missing),\n"
    "            \"due_missing\": sorted(raw_missing),\n"
    "            \"due_uncovered\": sorted(passive_uncovered),\n"
    "            \"reserved\": sorted(reserved),\n"
    "            \"claimed\": sorted(claimed),\n"
    "            \"future_missing\": [],\n"
    "            \"unscheduled_missing\": [],\n"
    "            \"off_day_missing\": [],\n"
    "            \"covered\": not bool(passive_uncovered),\n"
    "            \"next_episode\": int(strict_row.get(\"next_episode\") or 0),\n"
    "            \"next_air_at\": str(strict_row.get(\"next_air_at\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"next_precision\": str(strict_row.get(\"next_precision\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"passive_channel_bypass_v1125\": True,\n"
    "            \"strict_due_uncovered_v1125\": list(strict_row.get(\"due_uncovered\") or []),\n"
    "            \"strict_future_missing_v1125\": list(strict_row.get(\"future_missing\") or []),\n"
    "            \"strict_off_day_missing_v1125\": list(strict_row.get(\"off_day_missing\") or []),\n"
    "            \"checked_at\": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 5 分钟 tick：只保留频道刷新/事件匹配，旧 viewing poll 在本线程内返回空。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _tick_local_v1125(self):\n"
    "        local = getattr(self, \"_dispatch_tick_local_v1125\", None)\n"
    "        if local is None:\n"
    "            local = threading.local()\n"
    "            self._dispatch_tick_local_v1125 = local\n"
    "        return local\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _tick(self, host_service: bool = True) -> None:\n"
    "        local = self._tick_local_v1125()\n"
    "        previous = bool(getattr(local, \"channel_only\", False))\n"
    "        local.channel_only = True\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return super()._tick(host_service=host_service)\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            local.channel_only = previous\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 单一主动 Pull 选择器：先冷却，再日历；日历不可用才退回旧缺集语义。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _external_cooldown_due_v1125(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        sid: int,\n"
    "        state: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        now: float,\n"
    "    ) -> bool:\n"
    "        row = dict(state.get(str(sid)) or {})\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            last_at = float(row.get(\"last_at\") or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            last_at = 0.0\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            cooldown = max(900, int(getattr(self, \"_external_search_cooldown_minutes_v1114\", 180) or 180) * 60)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            cooldown = 180 * 60\n"
    "        return not last_at or now - last_at >= cooldown\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _smart_pull_due_ids_v1125(self) -> List[int]:\n"
    "        rows = self._active_selected_subscriptions_v1125()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            state = dict(self._external_search_state_v1114() or {})\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            state = {}\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "\n"
    "        cooldown_rows: List[Any] = []\n"
    "        for subscribe in rows:\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if sid > 0 and self._external_cooldown_due_v1125(sid, state, now):\n"
    "                cooldown_rows.append(subscribe)\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 没有任何订阅达到外部冷却时，不刷新日历，也不做额外媒体查询。\n"
    "        if not cooldown_rows:\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "\n"
    "        calendar: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None\n"
    "        if any(not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe) for subscribe in cooldown_rows):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                calendar = dict(self._refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(force=False) or {})\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                calendar = None\n"
    "\n"
    "        due: List[int] = []\n"
    "        fallback = 0\n"
    "        off_day = 0\n"
    "        for subscribe in cooldown_rows:\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if sid <= 0:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                if self._movie_needs_pull_v1125(subscribe):\n"
    "                    due.append(sid)\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "\n"
    "            uncovered = self._uncovered_missing_v1125(subscribe)\n"
    "            if not uncovered:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    with self._without_due_scope_v1120():\n"
    "                        if not self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe):\n"
    "                            self._finish_subscription_if_complete(subscribe)\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                if calendar is not None:\n"
    "                    gate = dict(self._airing_gate_v1120(subscribe, payload=calendar) or {})\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    gate = dict(self._airing_gate_v1120(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                gate = {}\n"
    "\n"
    "            if bool(gate.get(\"calendar_available\")):\n"
    "                if self._positive_ids_v1125(gate.get(\"due_uncovered\") or []):\n"
    "                    due.append(sid)\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    off_day += 1\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "\n"
    "            # 与既有 scheduler 保持一致：日历完全不可用时不能冻结追更，退回真实缺集 + 冷却。\n"
    "            fallback += 1\n"
    "            due.append(sid)\n"
    "\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self.save_data(\"dispatch_policy_v1125\", {\n"
    "                \"selector_at\": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n"
    "                \"active\": len(rows),\n"
    "                \"cooldown_due\": len(cooldown_rows),\n"
    "                \"pull_due\": len(due),\n"
    "                \"off_day\": off_day,\n"
    "                \"calendar_fallback\": fallback,\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        return sorted(set(due))\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _viewing_due_subscription_ids_v1115(self) -> List[int]:\n"
    "        local = self._tick_local_v1125()\n"
    "        if bool(getattr(local, \"channel_only\", False)):\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        return self._smart_pull_due_ids_v1125()\n"
    "\n"
    "    # 频道消息的异步 worker 结束后不再追加一次主动观影；历史遗留的“观影定时轮询”\n"
    "    # 若仍进入队列，也必须重新经过当前智能 selector，不能按旧大列表直接执行。\n"
    "    def _run_reliability_route_batch(self, batch: List[int], trigger: str) -> None:\n"
    "        text = str(trigger or \"\")\n"
    "        normalized = sorted(self._positive_ids_v1125(batch or []))\n"
    "        if \"频道新增资源\" in text:\n"
    "            if normalized:\n"
    "                return self._run_v1115_mode_batch(normalized, trigger, \"channel_event\", force=False)\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        if \"观影定时轮询\" in text:\n"
    "            allowed = set(self._smart_pull_due_ids_v1125())\n"
    "            filtered = [sid for sid in normalized if sid in allowed]\n"
    "            if filtered:\n"
    "                return self._run_v1115_mode_batch(filtered, \"更新日历主动拉取\", \"airing_pull\", force=False)\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        return super()._run_reliability_route_batch(batch, trigger)\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 每小时 AiringDue：唯一常规主动 GYING 时钟，严格服从外部检索冷却。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _calendar_due_check_v1110(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        active = self._active_selected_subscriptions_v1125()\n"
    "        due_ids = self._smart_pull_due_ids_v1125()\n"
    "        now_text = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")\n"
    "        state = self.get_data(\"airing_check_state_v1110\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(state, dict):\n"
    "            state = {}\n"
    "\n"
    "        results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for sid in due_ids:\n"
    "            subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "            episodes: List[int] = []\n"
    "            if subscribe and not self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    gate = dict(self._airing_gate_v1120(subscribe) or {})\n"
    "                    episodes = sorted(self._positive_ids_v1125(gate.get(\"due_uncovered\") or []))\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    episodes = []\n"
    "            state[str(sid)] = {\n"
    "                \"checked_at\": now_text,\n"
    "                \"episodes\": episodes,\n"
    "                \"mode\": \"airing_pull\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            results.append({\n"
    "                \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                \"episodes\": episodes,\n"
    "                \"scheduled\": True,\n"
    "                \"mode\": \"airing_pull\",\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        if len(state) > 1000:\n"
    "            state = dict(list(state.items())[-1000:])\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"airing_check_state_v1110\", state)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if due_ids:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【主动拉取】日历/电影冷却筛选后执行 %s 个订阅；5 分钟频道 tick 不再启动 GYING\",\n"
    "                len(due_ids),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self._run_v1115_mode_batch(\n"
    "                due_ids,\n"
    "                \"更新日历主动拉取\",\n"
    "                \"airing_pull\",\n"
    "                force=False,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"checked\": len(due_ids),\n"
    "            \"skipped\": max(0, len(active) - len(due_ids)),\n"
    "            \"results\": results,\n"
    "            \"mode\": \"single_airing_pull\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 每日 04:10：频道优先全量修复 -> 重算真实缺口 -> 仅剩余项 GYING 强制补漏。\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _repair_signature_v1125(self, subscribe: Any) -> Tuple[int, ...] | Tuple[str]:\n"
    "        if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "            return (\"movie\",) if self._movie_needs_pull_v1125(subscribe) else tuple()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            with self._without_due_scope_v1120():\n"
    "                return tuple(sorted(self._positive_ids_v1125(self._subscription_missing_episodes(subscribe) or [])))\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            return tuple()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _daily_full_catchup_v1110(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        started = datetime.datetime.now()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self.refresh_channels(force=True)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【每日全员复核】频道强制刷新失败，继续使用缓存：%s\",\n"
    "                str(err)[:260],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self._refresh_airing_calendar_v1120(force=True)\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【每日全员复核】更新日历刷新失败，仍继续两阶段修复：%s\",\n"
    "                str(err)[:260],\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        initial_rows = self._active_selected_subscriptions_v1125()\n"
    "        initial_ids = [int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0) for subscribe in initial_rows]\n"
    "        before: Dict[int, Tuple[Any, ...]] = {}\n"
    "        names: Dict[int, str] = {}\n"
    "        for subscribe in initial_rows:\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            names[sid] = str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                sync = getattr(self, \"_sync_media_library_progress\", None)\n"
    "                if callable(sync):\n"
    "                    sync(subscribe)\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "            fresh = self._find_subscription(sid) or subscribe\n"
    "            before[sid] = tuple(self._repair_signature_v1125(fresh))\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 第一阶段只消费频道现有资源；channel_event 会关闭 GYING，并由本层绕过日期门禁。\n"
    "        if initial_ids:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【每日全员复核】阶段1：%s 个订阅先消费频道资源，不访问 GYING\",\n"
    "                len(initial_ids),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self._run_v1115_mode_batch(\n"
    "                initial_ids,\n"
    "                \"每日全员复核·频道阶段\",\n"
    "                \"channel_event\",\n"
    "                force=False,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 重新读取成功回执 / reservation / source claim，只让真正仍未覆盖的订阅进入主动搜索。\n"
    "        remaining: List[int] = []\n"
    "        after_channel: Dict[int, Tuple[Any, ...]] = {}\n"
    "        for subscribe in self._active_selected_subscriptions_v1125():\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            fresh = self._find_subscription(sid) or subscribe\n"
    "            after_channel[sid] = tuple(self._repair_signature_v1125(fresh))\n"
    "            if self._is_movie_subscription(fresh):\n"
    "                if self._movie_needs_pull_v1125(fresh):\n"
    "                    remaining.append(sid)\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if self._uncovered_missing_v1125(fresh):\n"
    "                remaining.append(sid)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if remaining:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【每日全员复核】阶段2：频道后仍有 %s 个订阅存在未覆盖缺口，才进入 GYING 强制补漏\",\n"
    "                len(remaining),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self._run_v1115_mode_batch(\n"
    "                remaining,\n"
    "                \"每日全员复核·GYING阶段\",\n"
    "                \"daily_repair_pull\",\n"
    "                force=True,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        final_rows = {int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0): subscribe for subscribe in self._active_selected_subscriptions_v1125()}\n"
    "        results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        failed = changed = 0\n"
    "        remaining_set = set(remaining)\n"
    "        for sid in initial_ids:\n"
    "            subscribe = final_rows.get(sid) or self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "            if subscribe:\n"
    "                final_signature = tuple(self._repair_signature_v1125(subscribe))\n"
    "                if self._is_movie_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                    still_needs = self._movie_needs_pull_v1125(subscribe)\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    still_needs = bool(self._uncovered_missing_v1125(subscribe))\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                final_signature = tuple()\n"
    "                still_needs = False\n"
    "            if before.get(sid, tuple()) != final_signature:\n"
    "                changed += 1\n"
    "            if still_needs:\n"
    "                failed += 1\n"
    "            results.append({\n"
    "                \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                \"name\": names.get(sid, \"\"),\n"
    "                \"missing_before\": list(before.get(sid, tuple())),\n"
    "                \"missing_after_channel\": list(after_channel.get(sid, tuple())),\n"
    "                \"missing_after\": list(final_signature),\n"
    "                \"gying_attempted\": sid in remaining_set,\n"
    "                \"success\": not still_needs,\n"
    "            })\n"
    "\n"
    "        payload = {\n"
    "            \"started_at\": started.isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n"
    "            \"finished_at\": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\"),\n"
    "            \"checked\": len(initial_ids),\n"
    "            \"channel_phase\": len(initial_ids),\n"
    "            \"gying_phase\": len(remaining),\n"
    "            \"changed\": changed,\n"
    "            \"failed\": failed,\n"
    "            \"results\": results[-200:],\n"
    "            \"strategy\": \"channel_first_then_gying\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"daily_catchup_v1110\", payload)\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"INFO\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【每日全员复核】完成：订阅=%s 频道阶段=%s GYING阶段=%s 仍需补漏=%s\",\n"
    "            payload[\"checked\"],\n"
    "            payload[\"channel_phase\"],\n"
    "            payload[\"gying_phase\"],\n"
    "            payload[\"failed\"],\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"success\": True,\n"
    "            \"data\": payload,\n"
    "            \"message\": f\"已复核 {payload['checked']} 个订阅；仅 {payload['gying_phase']} 个进入 GYING 补漏\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaDispatchPolicyV1125Mixin\"]\n"
)

# ===== episode_compat_v171.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["episode_compat_v171"] = (
    "\"\"\"光鸭转存助手 v1.7.x 剧集弱命名兼容补丁。\n"
    "\n"
    "处理云盘分享中常见的“集号~画质”文件名，例如：\n"
    "01~4K.mp4、08～2160p.mkv、22~[4K][HEVC.AAC].mkv，以及\n"
    "01丨4K.mp4 / 01｜4K.mp4 / 01|4K.mp4 这类频道常见竖线分隔写法。\n"
    "\n"
    "补丁只在 legacy._episode_numbers 已完全无法识别时启用，并要求分隔符后必须是明确的\n"
    "画质/编码标记，避免把 01~04.mp4 这类可能表示范围的名字误判为第 1 集。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import functools\n"
    "import re\n"
    "from typing import Any, Optional\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_QUALITY_SUFFIX_EPISODE_PATTERN = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"\"\"(?ix)\n"
    "    ^\\s*0*(\\d{1,3})\\s*[~～丨|｜]\\s*\n"
    "    [\\[【(（]?\\s*\n"
    "    (?:\n"
    "        (?:4|8)K\n"
    "        |(?:2160|1080|720|480)[PI]?\n"
    "        |UHD|FHD|HD\n"
    "        |HDR(?:10\\+?)?\n"
    "        |DV|DOVI\n"
    "        |WEB(?:-?DL)?\n"
    "        |BLU-?RAY|BDREMUX|REMUX\n"
    "        |HEVC|AVC|AV1|H\\.?26[45]|X26[45]\n"
    "    )\n"
    "    (?=$|[\\s._\\-\\[\\]【】()（）])\n"
    "    \"\"\"\n"
    ")\n"
    "\n"
    "_UNPARSED_FAILURE_PATTERN = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"分享内有\\s*(\\d+)\\s*个媒体/字幕文件无法解析集号，未标记为已处理；示例：([^；\\n]+)\"\n"
    ")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def extract_quality_suffix_episode(path: Any) -> Optional[int]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"从“01~4K / 01丨4K”一类弱文件名中提取集号；不接受纯数字范围。\"\"\"\n"
    "    basename = str(path or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1]\n"
    "    matched = _QUALITY_SUFFIX_EPISODE_PATTERN.search(basename)\n"
    "    if not matched:\n"
    "        return None\n"
    "    episode = int(matched.group(1))\n"
    "    if 0 < episode <= 500 and episode not in {264, 265, 266}:\n"
    "        return episode\n"
    "    return None\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def install_episode_filename_compat(legacy_module: Any):\n"
    "    \"\"\"热重载安全地替换 legacy._episode_numbers。\"\"\"\n"
    "    current = getattr(legacy_module, \"_episode_numbers\", None)\n"
    "    if not callable(current):\n"
    "        return None\n"
    "    if getattr(current, \"_guangya_quality_suffix_compat\", False):\n"
    "        return current\n"
    "\n"
    "    @functools.wraps(current)\n"
    "    def patched(path: Any):\n"
    "        season, episodes = current(path)\n"
    "        if episodes:\n"
    "            return season, episodes\n"
    "        episode = extract_quality_suffix_episode(path)\n"
    "        if episode is None:\n"
    "            return season, episodes\n"
    "        return season, [episode]\n"
    "\n"
    "    patched._guangya_quality_suffix_compat = True\n"
    "    patched._guangya_original_episode_numbers = current\n"
    "    legacy_module._episode_numbers = patched\n"
    "    return patched\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def collapse_unparsed_failure_notice(text: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    \"\"\"同一通知里多条“无法解析集号”错误只保留一个合并摘要。\"\"\"\n"
    "    value = str(text or \"\")\n"
    "    matches = list(_UNPARSED_FAILURE_PATTERN.finditer(value))\n"
    "    if len(matches) <= 1:\n"
    "        return value\n"
    "\n"
    "    max_count = max(int(item.group(1)) for item in matches)\n"
    "    samples = []\n"
    "    seen = set()\n"
    "    for item in matches:\n"
    "        for sample in item.group(2).split(\"、\"):\n"
    "            sample = sample.strip()\n"
    "            if sample and sample not in seen:\n"
    "                seen.add(sample)\n"
    "                samples.append(sample)\n"
    "\n"
    "    merged = (\n"
    "        f\"分享内有 {max_count} 个媒体/字幕文件无法解析集号，未标记为已处理；\"\n"
    "        f\"示例：{'、'.join(samples[:8]) or '-'}\"\n"
    "    )\n"
    "    first = matches[0]\n"
    "    result = value[:first.start()] + merged + value[first.end():]\n"
    "\n"
    "    # 删除剩余同类片段，同时只吞掉与该片段相邻的一个中文分号。\n"
    "    while True:\n"
    "        remaining = list(_UNPARSED_FAILURE_PATTERN.finditer(result))\n"
    "        if len(remaining) <= 1:\n"
    "            break\n"
    "        item = remaining[1]\n"
    "        start, end = item.start(), item.end()\n"
    "        if start > 0 and result[start - 1] == \"；\":\n"
    "            start -= 1\n"
    "        elif end < len(result) and result[end] == \"；\":\n"
    "            end += 1\n"
    "        result = result[:start] + result[end:]\n"
    "    return result\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"extract_quality_suffix_episode\",\n"
    "    \"install_episode_filename_compat\",\n"
    "    \"collapse_unparsed_failure_notice\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== episode_resolver_v190.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["episode_resolver_v190"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.9.0 统一剧集识别器。\n"
    "\n"
    "目标不是“从文件名抓一个数字”，而是把文件名、父目录、整包序列与频道提示一起判断，\n"
    "给出可解释的 season / episodes / confidence。只有高置信结果才允许自动拆包。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import re\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE = 0.90\n"
    "MAX_EXPLICIT_EPISODE = 9999\n"
    "_COMMON_NOISE_NUMBERS = {264, 265, 266, 360, 480, 720, 1080, 2160, 4320}\n"
    "_VIDEO_SUFFIX_RE = re.compile(r\"\\.[A-Za-z0-9]{2,5}$\")\n"
    "_SEASON_RE = re.compile(r\"(?i)(?:^|[^A-Za-z0-9])S(?:eason)?[\\s._-]*0*(\\d{1,2})(?=[^0-9]|$)\")\n"
    "_X_RE = re.compile(r\"(?i)(?:^|[^0-9])0*(\\d{1,2})x0*(\\d{1,4})(?=[^0-9]|$)\")\n"
    "_EP_RANGE_RE = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"(?i)(?:^|[^A-Za-z])E(?:P(?:ISODE)?)?[\\s._-]*0*(\\d{1,4})\"\n"
    "    r\"(?:[\\s._]*(?:-|~|～|—|至|\\+)[\\s._]*E?(?:P(?:ISODE)?)?[\\s._-]*0*(\\d{1,4}))?\"\n"
    ")\n"
    "_CHINESE_RE = re.compile(r\"第\\s*0*(\\d{1,4})(?:\\s*[-~～—至]\\s*0*(\\d{1,4}))?\\s*[集话]\")\n"
    "_SPECIAL_RE = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"(?i)(?:^|[^A-Za-z0-9])(?:SP|SPECIAL|OVA|OAD)[\\s._-]*0*(\\d{1,4})(?=[^0-9]|$)\"\n"
    ")\n"
    "_CHINESE_SPECIAL_RE = re.compile(r\"(?:特别篇|番外|特典)\\s*0*(\\d{1,4})(?=[^0-9]|$)\")\n"
    "_QUALITY_TOKEN = (\n"
    "    r\"(?:4K|8K|2160P?|1080P?|720P?|480P?|UHD|FHD|HD|HDR(?:10\\+?)?|DV|DOVI|\"\n"
    "    r\"WEB(?:-?DL)?|BLU-?RAY|BDREMUX|REMUX|HEVC|AVC|AV1|H\\.?26[45]|X26[45])\"\n"
    ")\n"
    "_QUALITY_SUFFIX_RE = re.compile(\n"
    "    rf\"(?ix)^\\s*0*(\\d{{1,4}})\\s*[~～丨|｜]\\s*[\\[【(（]?\\s*{_QUALITY_TOKEN}\"\n"
    "    r\"(?=$|[\\s._\\-\\[\\]【】()（）])\"\n"
    ")\n"
    "_RELEASE_EP_BEFORE_QUALITY_RE = re.compile(\n"
    "    rf\"(?ix)(?:^|[\\s._\\-])0*(\\d{{1,4}})(?:v\\d+)?(?=[\\s._\\-]+{_QUALITY_TOKEN}(?:$|[\\s._\\-\\[\\]【】()（）]))\"\n"
    ")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _clean_path(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    return str(value or \"\").replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").strip()\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _basename(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    return _clean_path(value).rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _stem(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    return _VIDEO_SUFFIX_RE.sub(\"\", _basename(value)).strip()\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _expand_range(start: int, end: int) -> List[int]:\n"
    "    if start <= 0 or end < start or end > MAX_EXPLICIT_EPISODE or end - start > 500:\n"
    "        return []\n"
    "    return list(range(start, end + 1))\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _season_from_path(path: Any, explicit_hint: Optional[int] = None) -> Optional[int]:\n"
    "    if explicit_hint is not None:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return max(0, int(explicit_hint))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "    value = _clean_path(path)\n"
    "    matches = list(_SEASON_RE.finditer(value))\n"
    "    if matches:\n"
    "        return int(matches[-1].group(1))\n"
    "    return None\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _strict_parse(value: Any, season_hint: Optional[int] = None) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "    text = _clean_path(value)\n"
    "    season = _season_from_path(text, season_hint)\n"
    "    episodes = set()\n"
    "    reasons: List[str] = []\n"
    "\n"
    "    x_match = _X_RE.search(text)\n"
    "    if x_match:\n"
    "        if season is None:\n"
    "            season = int(x_match.group(1))\n"
    "        episodes.add(int(x_match.group(2)))\n"
    "        reasons.append(\"1x02\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    for matched in _EP_RANGE_RE.finditer(text):\n"
    "        start = int(matched.group(1))\n"
    "        end = int(matched.group(2)) if matched.group(2) else start\n"
    "        values = _expand_range(start, end)\n"
    "        if values:\n"
    "            episodes.update(values)\n"
    "            reasons.append(\"E/EP\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    for raw in re.findall(r\"(?i)E(?:P(?:ISODE)?)?[\\s._-]*0*(\\d{1,4})\", text):\n"
    "        value_int = int(raw)\n"
    "        if 0 < value_int <= MAX_EXPLICIT_EPISODE:\n"
    "            episodes.add(value_int)\n"
    "            reasons.append(\"multi-E\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    for matched in _CHINESE_RE.finditer(text):\n"
    "        start = int(matched.group(1))\n"
    "        end = int(matched.group(2)) if matched.group(2) else start\n"
    "        values = _expand_range(start, end)\n"
    "        if values:\n"
    "            episodes.update(values)\n"
    "            reasons.append(\"第N集/话\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    if episodes:\n"
    "        values = sorted(episode for episode in episodes if 0 < episode <= MAX_EXPLICIT_EPISODE)\n"
    "        absolute = values[0] if season is None and len(values) == 1 and values[0] > 500 else None\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"season\": season,\n"
    "            \"episodes\": values,\n"
    "            \"absolute_episode\": absolute,\n"
    "            \"confidence\": 1.0,\n"
    "            \"reason\": \"+\".join(dict.fromkeys(reasons)) or \"explicit\",\n"
    "            \"explicit\": True,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    special = _SPECIAL_RE.search(text) or _CHINESE_SPECIAL_RE.search(text)\n"
    "    if special:\n"
    "        value_int = int(special.group(1))\n"
    "        if 0 < value_int <= MAX_EXPLICIT_EPISODE:\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"season\": 0,\n"
    "                \"episodes\": [value_int],\n"
    "                \"absolute_episode\": None,\n"
    "                \"confidence\": 1.0,\n"
    "                \"reason\": \"special\",\n"
    "                \"explicit\": True,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "    return None\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _looks_like_year(value: int) -> bool:\n"
    "    return 1900 <= int(value) <= 2099\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _valid_weak_number(candidate: int) -> bool:\n"
    "    return bool(\n"
    "        0 < int(candidate) <= MAX_EXPLICIT_EPISODE\n"
    "        and int(candidate) not in _COMMON_NOISE_NUMBERS\n"
    "        and not _looks_like_year(int(candidate))\n"
    "    )\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _weak_numeric_candidate(value: Any) -> Optional[Tuple[int, str]]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"只返回位置语义明确的弱数字；不会从 1080p/H265/2026 中间硬抓数字。\"\"\"\n"
    "    stem = _stem(value)\n"
    "    quality = _QUALITY_SUFFIX_RE.search(stem)\n"
    "    if quality:\n"
    "        candidate = int(quality.group(1))\n"
    "        if _valid_weak_number(candidate):\n"
    "            return candidate, \"quality-suffix\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    release_quality = list(_RELEASE_EP_BEFORE_QUALITY_RE.finditer(stem))\n"
    "    if release_quality:\n"
    "        candidate = int(release_quality[-1].group(1))\n"
    "        if _valid_weak_number(candidate):\n"
    "            return candidate, \"release-before-quality\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    patterns: Sequence[Tuple[str, str]] = (\n"
    "        (r\"[\\[【(（]\\s*0*(\\d{1,4})(?:v\\d+)?\\s*[\\]】)）]\", \"bracket-number\"),\n"
    "        (r\"(?:^|[\\s._])[-–—][\\s._-]*0*(\\d{1,4})(?:v\\d+)?(?=\\s|[._\\[(（]|$)\", \"dash-number\"),\n"
    "        (r\"^\\s*0*(\\d{1,4})(?:v\\d+)?$\", \"bare-number\"),\n"
    "        (r\"^\\s*0*(\\d{1,4})(?:v\\d+)?(?=[\\s._\\-\\[(（])\", \"leading-number\"),\n"
    "        (r\"(?:^|[\\s._-])0*(\\d{1,4})(?:v\\d+)?$\", \"trailing-number\"),\n"
    "    )\n"
    "    for pattern, reason in patterns:\n"
    "        matched = re.search(pattern, stem, re.I)\n"
    "        if not matched:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        candidate = int(matched.group(1))\n"
    "        if not _valid_weak_number(candidate):\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        return candidate, reason\n"
    "    return None\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _package_sequence_numbers(paths: Iterable[Any]) -> List[int]:\n"
    "    values = []\n"
    "    seen = set()\n"
    "    for path in paths or []:\n"
    "        strict = _strict_parse(path)\n"
    "        if strict and strict.get(\"episodes\"):\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        weak = _weak_numeric_candidate(path)\n"
    "        if not weak:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        candidate = int(weak[0])\n"
    "        if candidate not in seen:\n"
    "            seen.add(candidate)\n"
    "            values.append(candidate)\n"
    "    return sorted(values)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _is_coherent_sequence(values: Sequence[int]) -> bool:\n"
    "    if len(values) < 3:\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "    ordered = sorted(set(int(v) for v in values))\n"
    "    span = ordered[-1] - ordered[0] + 1\n"
    "    return span <= len(ordered) + 1 and span <= 500\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def resolve_episode(\n"
    "    path: Any,\n"
    "    *,\n"
    "    package_paths: Optional[Iterable[Any]] = None,\n"
    "    season_hint: Optional[int] = None,\n"
    "    episode_hint: Any = \"\",\n"
    ") -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"返回可解释的剧集识别结果；低于阈值只诊断，不自动拆包。\"\"\"\n"
    "    strict = _strict_parse(path, season_hint=season_hint)\n"
    "    if strict:\n"
    "        return strict\n"
    "\n"
    "    weak = _weak_numeric_candidate(path)\n"
    "    if not weak:\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"season\": _season_from_path(path, season_hint),\n"
    "            \"episodes\": [],\n"
    "            \"absolute_episode\": None,\n"
    "            \"confidence\": 0.0,\n"
    "            \"reason\": \"unparsed\",\n"
    "            \"explicit\": False,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    candidate, weak_reason = weak\n"
    "    season = _season_from_path(path, season_hint)\n"
    "    confidence = 0.65\n"
    "    reason = weak_reason\n"
    "\n"
    "    hinted = _strict_parse(episode_hint, season_hint=season) if episode_hint else None\n"
    "    if hinted and candidate in set(hinted.get(\"episodes\") or []):\n"
    "        confidence = max(confidence, 0.96)\n"
    "        reason += \"+episode-hint\"\n"
    "        if season is None:\n"
    "            season = hinted.get(\"season\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    package_numbers = _package_sequence_numbers(package_paths or [])\n"
    "    if candidate in package_numbers and _is_coherent_sequence(package_numbers):\n"
    "        confidence = max(confidence, 0.92)\n"
    "        reason += \"+package-sequence\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    if season is not None and weak_reason in {\n"
    "        \"bare-number\", \"leading-number\", \"trailing-number\", \"bracket-number\", \"dash-number\",\n"
    "        \"quality-suffix\", \"release-before-quality\",\n"
    "    }:\n"
    "        confidence = max(confidence, 0.90)\n"
    "        reason += \"+season-context\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    absolute = candidate if season is None and candidate > 500 else None\n"
    "    return {\n"
    "        \"season\": season,\n"
    "        \"episodes\": [candidate],\n"
    "        \"absolute_episode\": absolute,\n"
    "        \"confidence\": round(confidence, 3),\n"
    "        \"reason\": reason,\n"
    "        \"explicit\": False,\n"
    "    }\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def reliable_episode_set(result: Dict[str, Any], threshold: float = AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE) -> set[int]:\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        confidence = float(result.get(\"confidence\") or 0.0)\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        confidence = 0.0\n"
    "    if confidence < float(threshold):\n"
    "        return set()\n"
    "    values = set()\n"
    "    for raw in result.get(\"episodes\") or []:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            value = int(raw)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        if 0 < value <= MAX_EXPLICIT_EPISODE:\n"
    "            values.add(value)\n"
    "    return values\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"AUTO_SELECT_CONFIDENCE\",\n"
    "    \"MAX_EXPLICIT_EPISODE\",\n"
    "    \"resolve_episode\",\n"
    "    \"reliable_episode_set\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== episode_runtime_v211.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["episode_runtime_v211"] = (
    "\"\"\"2.0.11-r95 P0：Episode Runtime Architecture — observation ≠ receipt ≠ reservation.\n"
    "\n"
    "Breaks Library→Receipt→Missing→Target recursion; migrates polluted library-origin\n"
    "facts; AiringDue host heartbeat + runtime fallback singleflight; empty-target submit gate.\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import threading\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from contextlib import contextmanager\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, Iterator, List, Optional, Set, Tuple\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_LIBRARY_ORIGINS = frozenset({\n"
    "    \"library\", \"emby\", \"mediaserver\", \"scan\", \"media_library\", \"library_observation\",\n"
    "})\n"
    "_MIGRATION_MARKER = \"episode_facts_migration_v211\"\n"
    "_ACTIVE_INFLIGHT_STATES = frozenset({\n"
    "    \"new\", \"dispatching\", \"queued\", \"submitted\", \"waiting\", \"verifying\",\n"
    "    \"task_confirmed\", \"retry\", \"pending\", \"running\",\n"
    "})\n"
    "_TLS = threading.local()\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _positive(values: Any) -> Set[int]:\n"
    "    out: Set[int] = set()\n"
    "    for raw in values or []:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            episode = int(raw)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        if episode > 0:\n"
    "            out.add(episode)\n"
    "    return out\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _is_library_origin(origin: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "    text = str(origin or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "    if not text:\n"
    "        return False\n"
    "    if text in _LIBRARY_ORIGINS:\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "    return any(token in text for token in (\"library\", \"emby\", \"mediaserver\", \"scan\"))\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _context_stack() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "    stack = getattr(_TLS, \"episode_run_contexts\", None)\n"
    "    if not isinstance(stack, list):\n"
    "        stack = []\n"
    "        _TLS.episode_run_contexts = stack\n"
    "    return stack\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaEpisodeRuntimeV211Mixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"Runtime supervisor + claim lifecycle + migration + empty-target hard gate.\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    _airing_fallback_stale_seconds_v211 = 15 * 60\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        # Runtime structures BEFORE super() so workers cannot race into facts builders.\n"
    "        self._episode_runtime_ready_v211 = False\n"
    "        self._airing_due_lock_v211 = threading.RLock()\n"
    "        self._airing_due_running_v211 = False\n"
    "        self._airing_due_owner_v211 = \"\"\n"
    "        self._airing_due_cycle_v211 = 0\n"
    "        self._host_airing_heartbeat_v211 = 0.0\n"
    "        self._episode_snapshot_tls_v211 = threading.local()\n"
    "        self._airing_cycle_state_v211: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n"
    "        if hasattr(self, \"_library_sync_reuse_v211\"):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                delattr(self, \"_library_sync_reuse_v211\")\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                self._library_sync_reuse_v211 = None\n"
    "        super().init_plugin(config=config)\n"
    "        self._migrate_library_observation_facts_v211_once()\n"
    "        self._reopen_library_polluted_superseded_sources_v211()\n"
    "        self._episode_runtime_ready_v211 = True\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _episode_runtime_is_ready_v211(self) -> bool:\n"
    "        return bool(getattr(self, \"_episode_runtime_ready_v211\", False))\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # AiringDueCycleContext — selector snapshot → transfer reuse\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _airing_cycle_ensure_v211(self, cycle: int, owner: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        state = {\n"
    "            \"cycle_id\": int(cycle or 0),\n"
    "            \"owner\": str(owner or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"started_at\": time.time(),\n"
    "            \"phase\": \"select\",\n"
    "            \"per_sid\": {},\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        self._airing_cycle_state_v211 = state\n"
    "        return state\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _airing_cycle_current_v211(self) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        state = getattr(self, \"_airing_cycle_state_v211\", None)\n"
    "        return state if isinstance(state, dict) and state.get(\"cycle_id\") else None\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _airing_cycle_clear_v211(self) -> None:\n"
    "        self._airing_cycle_state_v211 = {}\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _airing_cycle_store_seed_v211(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        ctx: Dict[str, Any],\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        gate_result: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n"
    "    ) -> None:\n"
    "        state = self._airing_cycle_current_v211()\n"
    "        if not state:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        if sid <= 0:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        cycle = int(state.get(\"cycle_id\") or 0)\n"
    "        seed = dict(ctx or {})\n"
    "        run_id = str(seed.get(\"run_id\") or \"\") or f\"run:#{sid}:airing:{cycle}\"\n"
    "        seed[\"run_id\"] = run_id\n"
    "        seed[\"sid\"] = sid\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            seed[\"season\"] = int(getattr(subscribe, \"season\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            seed[\"season\"] = 0\n"
    "        if isinstance(gate_result, dict):\n"
    "            seed[\"gate_result\"] = dict(gate_result)\n"
    "            seed[\"final_target\"] = list(gate_result.get(\"final_target\") or seed.get(\"final_target\") or [])\n"
    "            seed[\"mp_missing\"] = list(gate_result.get(\"mp_missing\") or seed.get(\"mp_missing\") or [])\n"
    "        seed[\"selector_done\"] = True\n"
    "        state.setdefault(\"per_sid\", {})[sid] = seed\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _airing_cycle_seed_for_subscribe_v211(self, subscribe: Any) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        state = self._airing_cycle_current_v211()\n"
    "        if not state:\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        row = (state.get(\"per_sid\") or {}).get(sid)\n"
    "        return dict(row) if isinstance(row, dict) else None\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _airing_cycle_cached_gate_v211(self, subscribe: Any) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"Second gate call in same AiringDue cycle returns selector result (no Emby #2).\"\"\"\n"
    "        seed = self._airing_cycle_seed_for_subscribe_v211(subscribe)\n"
    "        if not seed or not seed.get(\"selector_done\"):\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        gate = seed.get(\"gate_result\")\n"
    "        return dict(gate) if isinstance(gate, dict) else None\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _begin_subscription_run_v209(self, subscribe: Any) -> str:\n"
    "        \"\"\"Stable run:#<sid>:airing:<cycle> while AiringDue cycle is active.\"\"\"\n"
    "        state = self._airing_cycle_current_v211()\n"
    "        if state and bool(getattr(self, \"_airing_due_running_v211\", False)):\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            cycle = int(state.get(\"cycle_id\") or 0)\n"
    "            seed = (state.get(\"per_sid\") or {}).get(sid) or {}\n"
    "            run_id = str(seed.get(\"run_id\") or \"\") or f\"run:#{sid}:airing:{cycle}\"\n"
    "            tls = self._diag_tls_v209()\n"
    "            previous = getattr(tls, \"stack\", None)\n"
    "            if not isinstance(previous, list):\n"
    "                previous = []\n"
    "            previous.append({\n"
    "                \"run_id\": getattr(tls, \"run_id\", \"\"),\n"
    "                \"sid\": getattr(tls, \"sid\", 0),\n"
    "                \"candidate_diags\": list(getattr(tls, \"candidate_diags\", []) or []),\n"
    "                \"episode_claims\": dict(getattr(tls, \"episode_claims\", {}) or {}),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            tls.stack = previous\n"
    "            tls.run_id = run_id\n"
    "            tls.sid = sid\n"
    "            tls.candidate_diags = []\n"
    "            tls.episode_claims = {}\n"
    "            self._current_subscription_run_id_v209 = run_id\n"
    "            self._current_subscription_candidate_diags_v209 = tls.candidate_diags\n"
    "            return run_id\n"
    "        return super()._begin_subscription_run_v209(subscribe)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _mp_authoritative_missing_episodes_v209(self, subscribe: Any):\n"
    "        \"\"\"Cache MP missing once per EpisodeFactsContext / AiringDue sid seed.\"\"\"\n"
    "        ctx = self._episode_run_context_for_subscribe_v211(subscribe)\n"
    "        if ctx is not None and ctx.get(\"mp_missing_resolved\"):\n"
    "            return str(ctx.get(\"mp_missing_state\") or \"used_missing\"), set(\n"
    "                int(v) for v in (ctx.get(\"mp_missing\") or []) if int(v or 0) > 0\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        seed = self._airing_cycle_seed_for_subscribe_v211(subscribe)\n"
    "        if seed and seed.get(\"mp_missing_resolved\"):\n"
    "            return str(seed.get(\"mp_missing_state\") or \"used_missing\"), set(\n"
    "                int(v) for v in (seed.get(\"mp_missing\") or []) if int(v or 0) > 0\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        source, missing = super()._mp_authoritative_missing_episodes_v209(subscribe)\n"
    "        payload = {\n"
    "            \"mp_missing_resolved\": True,\n"
    "            \"mp_missing_state\": str(source or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"mp_missing\": sorted(int(v) for v in (missing or set()) if int(v or 0) > 0),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if ctx is not None:\n"
    "            ctx.update(payload)\n"
    "            ctx[\"mp_resolve_count\"] = int(ctx.get(\"mp_resolve_count\") or 0) + 1\n"
    "        if seed is not None:\n"
    "            seed.update(payload)\n"
    "            state = self._airing_cycle_current_v211()\n"
    "            if state:\n"
    "                sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "                state.setdefault(\"per_sid\", {})[sid] = seed\n"
    "        return source, set(missing or set())\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _gying_raw_results(self, keyword: str, force: bool = False):\n"
    "        \"\"\"One business-level GYING search per run+keyword+targets (HTTP may still multi-hit).\"\"\"\n"
    "        ctx = self._current_episode_run_context_v211()\n"
    "        key = None\n"
    "        if ctx is not None:\n"
    "            targets = tuple(sorted(int(v) for v in (ctx.get(\"final_target\") or []) if int(v or 0) > 0))\n"
    "            key = (str(ctx.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"), str(keyword or \"\").strip(), targets)\n"
    "            cache = ctx.setdefault(\"gying_search_cache\", {})\n"
    "            if key in cache and not force:\n"
    "                rows, state = cache[key]\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【观影】搜索结果复用 run=%s keyword=%s target=%s result_count=%s\",\n"
    "                    key[0] or \"-\",\n"
    "                    key[1][:80] or \"-\",\n"
    "                    \",\".join(f\"E{v:02d}\" for v in targets) or \"-\",\n"
    "                    len(rows or []),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                return list(rows or []), dict(state or {})\n"
    "        rows, state = super()._gying_raw_results(keyword, force=force)\n"
    "        if ctx is not None and key is not None:\n"
    "            ctx.setdefault(\"gying_search_cache\", {})[key] = (list(rows or []), dict(state or {}))\n"
    "            ctx[\"gying_business_search_count\"] = int(ctx.get(\"gying_business_search_count\") or 0) + 1\n"
    "        return rows, state\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _try_transfer_subscription(self, subscribe: Any, force: bool = False, refresh_channel: bool = True):\n"
    "        \"\"\"Re-enter selector EpisodeFactsContext for the same AiringDue cycle.\"\"\"\n"
    "        if not self._episode_runtime_is_ready_v211():\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"handled\": False, \"message\": \"episode_runtime_not_ready\"}\n"
    "        seed = self._airing_cycle_seed_for_subscribe_v211(subscribe)\n"
    "        if seed:\n"
    "            state = self._airing_cycle_current_v211() or {}\n"
    "            if state:\n"
    "                state[\"phase\"] = \"transfer\"\n"
    "            run_id = str(seed.get(\"run_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            with self._episode_run_context_scope_v211(subscribe, run_id=run_id, seed=seed):\n"
    "                return super()._try_transfer_subscription(\n"
    "                    subscribe, force=force, refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "        return super()._try_transfer_subscription(\n"
    "            subscribe, force=force, refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # Per-run EpisodeFactsContext (thread-local — never instance-shared)\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _run_id_v210(self) -> str:\n"
    "        ctx = self._current_episode_run_context_v211()\n"
    "        if isinstance(ctx, dict) and str(ctx.get(\"run_id\") or \"\").strip():\n"
    "            return str(ctx.get(\"run_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "        return super()._run_id_v210()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _current_episode_run_context_v211(self) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        stack = _context_stack()\n"
    "        return stack[-1] if stack else None\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _episode_run_context_for_subscribe_v211(self, subscribe: Any) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
